## Volume I — Theoretical Core

> **This volume = Volume I.**

**Claim of this volume.** The persistence of a persistent structure can be written as an auditable ledger: the accumulation rate of net effective causal information $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ is offset against the baseline depreciation rate $\gamma_0$, and this fixes the equilibrium position of the viability potential $V_{\mathrm{obj}}=V_{\mathrm{s}}+V_{\mathrm{c}}$. The same physical signal can be booked as $I^{+}$, $I^{-}$ or $I^{0}$ by different decoders, so the way the ledger is kept varies with the decoder; what an active-phase structure actually optimizes is $V^{\mathrm{val}}$ — the value viability potential, measured by the virtual signal $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$ — and not $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$. Along this thread, the volume gives the Variable Constitution with dimensional bookkeeping, the triple splitting of the viability potential, a reduced $I$–$V$ subsystem whose equilibrium point and saddle-node bifurcation threshold both have closed forms, eleven framework propositions each with its own refutation condition, and an executable pre-registered falsification protocol.

**Limits of use.** The items above are labeled one by one with the four evidence levels (**Theorem**, **Proposition**, **Candidate Corollary**, **Hypothesis**), and the premises and failure conditions are stated at the point of use. All values and orders of magnitude in the text are illustrative model calculations, not calibrated clinically or empirically, and they yield no prognostic reading at the level of any individual. The full limits of use, the measurement contract and the ethical constraints are given in §10.5.

***

**Introduction**

Before the strict argument begins, two objects serve to show the core concepts intuitively.

A deserted island. One man, one python, one wild boar.

The boar meets the man first. It has never seen this species: too big to be prey, too slow to be a threat. In its decoder the man is only a lump of irrelevant noise — **$I^0$**. It ignores him and goes on foraging at its leisure.

The man notices the boar at the same moment: it is hard to catch directly, so it yields no positive-effect information $I^+$; its tusks are sharp, so it carries the risk of negative-effect information $I^-$. He chooses the safest course — he climbs a tree.

Deep in the canopy a python is already coiled. It takes the man and the boar into view together: the boar is a lump of high-value protein, a good $I^+$; the man is a strange species, and whether his effect is positive or negative is far from clear, so unless it is extremely hungry he is not on the priority menu.

The boar smells the snake first, and fear seizes it: its **$T_{\mathrm{pred}}$**, the endogenous time horizon, contracts sharply until only the life-or-death of this moment is left. It bolts for a kilometer, the sense of danger fades, and it lowers its head to root in the soil again. For the boar, a threat that has left the range of perception is as good as never having existed.

The man slides down the tree and flees. But once he is out of danger, his **$T_{\mathrm{pred}}$** does not stop at "safe for now"; it keeps extending forward — to tomorrow, to the day after, and to the more distant future. In the future he predicts, the snake is still there, and the deadly threat is still there.

For the boar, the future does not exist; for the man, the future has already begun to affect the present.

So the man acts, and the higher-order recursion of $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ (net effective causal information) is set in motion. "Traps", "spears", "vine ropes" — objects not yet present in the physical world — take shape in his accumulated knowledge and in his rehearsal of the future, and are then made by his hands. He also realizes that in close combat, fleeing and fighting alike would burn enormous physical strength, and that once $E_{\mathrm{thresh}}$ (the damage threshold) is touched he is finished; so in advance he spends the $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ of his idle hours on training his body, in exchange for more usable strength in the future.

On the second day the snake finds the boar, and a predation with no suspense takes place. On the third day the man finds the snake's nest and steals its eggs.

When the snake returns to its nest, it finds the man eating the last few eggs. In the snake's decoder the man jumps from a strange species to a strong $I^-$ that directly threatens the persistence of its own genetic PS, and it attacks at once. What meets it is a set of tactics and weapons far more complex than its cognitive model. The snake is wounded and slithers away with a severed tail; the man too is mortally wounded in the fight.

Dying, he thinks of his family at home, of ambitions unfulfilled, and of the snake that is about to bring his **$V_{\mathrm{s}}$** to zero. In the operable space that remains to him, he bears the pain and carves a will into the rock: he writes down what he wanted to say to his family and never said, divides his inheritance, and asks relatives and friends to kill the snake for him.

Having cut the last stroke, he has no strength left to move another step. The wound keeps bleeding; hunger and cold together consume what resources he has left: the contribution of the present causal channel to maintaining his persistence, $V_{\mathrm{c}}$, falls steadily and keeps eroding the structural viability reserve $V_{\mathrm{s}}$ that is not yet exhausted. When the objective viability potential $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ drops below the sustainable threshold, and the remaining operable state space contains no feasible plan that could pull it back, what he can do narrows sharply — narrows to nothing but breathing. Before dawn he dies of blood loss and hypothermia. Structural wear has run through its account a step ahead of him: $T_{\mathrm{struct}}$ was used up before he had finished thinking things through.

Three months later another man finds the rock bearing the will. The will enters legal proceedings, and that textual information, with its handwritten signature and distinctive handwriting, begins to exert causal efficacy $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ on the physical world in his place, carrying out what he left unfinished: dissolving the estrangement from his family, bringing wealth to the family PS, and winning more reachable future space for his genetic PS. Relatives and friends hunt the snake for him and raise a monument to him on the island. His name, likeness, deeds and last wishes are preserved; the personal PS turns into a memetic PS, continues to persist in the physical world, and extends its own **$T_{\mathrm{pred}}$** as far as it can.

The same set of conditions can be seen more clearly in a nonliving object. On a coastline stands a lighthouse, in service for one hundred and forty years. Its value has never lain in the brick and stone themselves but in the sea lane it lit: the fixed flash cycle each night is a tacit agreement between it and every ship that passed. Later the lane shifted, and freight moved to a deep-water port farther away; the light no longer corresponded to any real need for persistence. Salt spray set in — rebar rusted and swelled, masonry effloresced — and the annual maintenance budget slid from "routine repair" to "overall reinforcement" and then to "demolition and rebuilding". The assessment report put the rebuilding cost beyond all the resources the responsible agency could mobilize within one fiscal period; phasing the work over several years would require the lighthouse to stand out of service for several years in a row, and the substitute arrangements for that period would themselves need new capital outlay. The repair plan thus fell outside the operable space — the cost of every feasible plan lay outside the available budget. The lighthouse was decommissioned by a procedure written in advance: the light put out, the lens and rotating mechanism removed, the staircase sealed, the maritime authority notified to update the charts, and the logbook, the optical drawings and the keepers' journals handed to the local archive. It was entered on the register of heritage sites; in summer tourists climb up to take photographs, and an information board states its flash cycle and the origin of that agreement. The brick and stone remain, but they persist in a different topology: from "a functional structure that keeps emitting light signals" to "a historical structure that is read again and again". This is a structure, under given energy and time constraints, switching its remaining operable space from "maintaining the original function" to "continuing to be read in another way".

***

In this story the boar, the python, the man, the spear, the will, the money and the descendants are not the true protagonists. **The true protagonist is the negentropic configuration that, under a given energy constraint, maintains its own ordered topological features — the persistent structure** (PS).

### What Is a Persistent Structure?

The universe obeys the law of entropy increase, and everything ends by tending toward disorder. Yet in this irreversible flood, islands of order keep emerging that swim against the current: crystals, stars, cells, neural networks, language, institutions, civilizations, and a will that carries meaning. These apparently unrelated things share one essence: each is a specific ordered informational structure that, supported by some energy, can be identified against background noise. SRVD (Structure Recursive Viability Dynamics) holds that when any object in the universe shows identifiable ordered features, its persistence and its demise can be brought under one cross-scale analytical framework:

$$
V \approx \frac{I \cdot T}{E} \tag{I-0.1}
$$

**Viability potential = information × time ÷ energy cost.**

> **$V$ is the formal notation for the viability potential (a bare $V$ means $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$); $V\approx I\cdot T/E$ is its low-order approximation, and also the unified public statement of this framework.** Channel-by-channel accounting $V_{\mathrm{obj}}=V_{\mathrm{s}}+V_{\mathrm{c}}$ (Equation (I-3.2)) is a **bookkeeping decomposition**: the "+" in it is a provisional notation, and the aggregation operator (addition, taking the maximum, or a more complex trade-off) is not yet determined, so it carries no definitional function. Wherever $V\approx I\cdot T/E$ is used, it must be marked as a low-order approximation with its calibration status declared; wherever $V_{\mathrm{s}}+V_{\mathrm{c}}$ is used, the provisional status of the aggregation operator must be declared.

Here $I$ (information processing and causal efficacy), $T$ (time horizon) and $E$ (thermodynamic cost) are all generalized variables, which can be unfolded into concrete dynamical indicators in different systems. The viability potential $V$ is an integral measure of the persistence strength of a persistent structure, and is the core state variable that is continually optimized in the course of evolution. We take ourselves to be pursuing happiness, wealth, power, meaning, love, glory or revenge, but under the unified view of thermodynamics and information science, these motives can all be tentatively reinterpreted as different expressions, under different constraints, of one common measure — the **viability potential**; "maximization" holds only in the active phase (§3.5, §5.7) and is not asserted as a driving claim about all structures.

### The Structure of This Book

This book consists of five volumes, each standing as an independent book, with no volume depending on another in reading order. This volume carries the theoretical core: it establishes the variable system, the core dynamics and the falsifiable predictions — how a persistent structure is defined; relative to whom its causal efficacy holds (that is, the decoder $D$); why only higher-order intelligences produce complex "love and hate" (that is, the triple splitting of $V$); why intelligence brings the risk of myopic runaway; and how to give early warning.

### A Four-Level Placement

Every statement in this volume falls on one of the four layers below. When reading, first judge which layer a sentence is on, then judge how hard it is.

- **Ontological layer**: persistent structures $P$ and the physical channel — **what exists**;
- **Axiomatic layer**: the low-order coarse-grained baseline of the viability potential, $V\approx I\cdot T/E$ — **what measures**. It is selected under a specified approximation and is not derived from the laws of physics; the reasons and failure conditions are given in §3.1;
- **Driving layer**: $\max V$ — **the driving force of evolution**. In the passive phase it describes the outcome of selection (purposeless); in the active phase it is an endogenously driven goal (purposive); the two levels must not be conflated. ($\max V$ denotes two mathematical objects in this book: the selection-layer inequality $\mathbb{E}[V_{\text{retained}}]\ge\mathbb{E}[V_{\text{eliminated}}]$, defined in Volume II §7.5; and the objective-layer $\max V^{\mathrm{val}}$, see §3.5 and Framework Proposition 1. They share one ruler, but different layers must not be conflated, nor substituted for each other. Any $\max V$ not accompanied by "within the constraint region (I-5.19)" is to be read as shorthand for the latter.)
- **Mechanism layer**: the decoder $D$ — **how a structure exchanges with the world**.

The four layers stand in dependence relations, not a ranking of "which is more fundamental": $D\to I\to V$ is a chain of definitional dependence, and $\max V$ is a teleological statement; the two belong to different dimensions. Wherever this volume's conclusions fall short in strength, the layer at which they stop is noted.

### Division of Labor with Adjacent Frameworks

This volume and the adjacent *Relational Information Framework* (DOI: 10.5281/zenodo.24365795) each stand independently and can each be read alone. The latter defines "information" strictly as how rare a reading is relative to some background, and writes out in full the whole set of measurement-layer problems: background construction, reading protocol, defect registration and unit bookkeeping; it does not discuss persistence, and gives no composite formula for viability efficiency. This volume adopts the information definition it gives at the conceptual level, is fully self-contained at the level of dynamics, and, on top of the three quantities information, time and energy, discusses the composition and dynamics of the viability potential: how information is given a polarity by a decoder, how the time horizon is endogenized, how energy cost is apportioned between two channels, and which phases appear when the three couple.

The two books share names and meanings for six concepts — persistent structure, decoder, structural information, causal information, the two kinds of persistence time, and the two energy channels — and each gives complete definitions within itself: this volume's Chapter 1 defines the persistent structure and the decoder, and Chapter 2 defines the three families of information, time and energy. Apart from this one statement, this volume depends on no external text; its notation and criteria are all supplied by the volume itself.

### Why This Framework Is Needed

We are living in an era of high-speed co-evolution between silicon-based AI and carbon-based civilization; memes, algorithms, institutions and organisms are entangled with one another, and the old disciplinary boundaries no longer suffice to describe this kind of cross-scale hybrid. SRVD's core innovation has two parts: it proposes the two-channel definition of the viability potential and its low-order coarse-grained baseline $V\approx I\cdot T/E$ as a candidate cross-scale unifying **metric language**; and it gives a **driving-layer meta-postulate** — under given constraints, the structures that are retained have a viability potential no lower than those that are eliminated (selection layer, purposeless, refutable by statistical observation); for active-phase structures, $\max V^{\mathrm{val}}$ is at the same time the direction actually optimized (objective layer, purposive, refutable by the direction of strategy drift). The two layers must not be conflated. The sentence "all other mechanisms are merely local strategies" does not hold: at the level of local strategy, which route to take is a real choice; the selection layer does not change the standard but only selects among the consequences of these choices (§3.3.2, Volume II §7.5).

SRVD violates no known law of physics and does not rebuild the foundations of any existing scientific theory; it offers only another lens with penetrating power: putting minerals, life, machines, civilizations and memes at the same table, seeing what laws they share and where they differ in essence, and so judging what futures are possible and how much operable space we still have.

***

## Abstract

Structure Recursive Viability Dynamics (SRVD) is a cross-scale, macroscopic, coarse-grained framework that brings all persistent structures — crystals, living systems, AI models, civilizations, memes — under one information–time–energy description. Axiom: **without a decoder, there is no information (as effective causal information).**

The core measure is the viability potential $V$. The single-channel coarse-grained baseline form $V \approx I \cdot T / E$ corresponds to the special case $\alpha=\beta=\gamma=1$ and does not constitute the definition of $V$; the general coarse-grained response form is the power law $V = V_0 (I/I_0)^{\alpha} (T/T_0)^{\beta} (E/E_0)^{-\gamma}$, in which the three ratios are dimensionless and $V_0$ has the same dimension as $V$; with bit, s and J as units, the baseline $V$ has dimension bit·s/J. The elasticity exponents $\alpha, \beta, \gamma$ are retained as parameters to be determined; the ordering $\alpha/\gamma > 1 > \beta/\gamma$ is a qualitative conjecture based on relative resource scarcity, awaiting derivation or empirical test.

There are four main contents. First, **endogenization of the decoder**: the value of information is not an intrinsic property of the signal but is extracted by the decoder $D$, which also assigns its polarity ($I^{+}/I^{-}/I^{0}$); the cognitive decoder $D_{\mathrm{cog}}$ is a three-axis continuous space plus a fourth, self-referential axis, and the embodied, online and symbolic types are regions, not discrete classes. Second, **the triple splitting of the viability potential**: the objective viability potential is aggregated at the model level as $V_{\mathrm{obj}}=V_{\mathrm{s}}+V_{\mathrm{c}}$, which requires first fixing the accounting boundary, observation window, holder attribution, cost apportionment and effect de-duplication; the low-order $V\approx I\cdot T/E$ is the low-order approximation of $V$ and its public statement; $V_{\mathrm{obj}}=V_{\mathrm{s}}+V_{\mathrm{c}}$ is a bookkeeping decomposition whose aggregation operator is yet to be determined. Once $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ can regulate itself, the system further distinguishes $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$, $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$ and $V^{\mathrm{val}}$; the splitting itself is neutral and is a necessary condition for higher-order cognition; after it there are at least two paths, depending on the decoupling deviation $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$ and the self-referential coupling strength $\chi$ — one puts the virtual potential to use for long-term investment and feedforward regulation, the other is the myopic runaway regime, which can be activated only in higher-order systems in the symbolic region and is not an inevitable fate. For a simplified information–viability-potential subsystem, this text gives an analytic treatment of equilibrium points and stability, including explicit formulas for the saddle-node bifurcation and the critical coupling strength; this result belongs to the specified subsystem, and its correspondence to the thresholds of a full cognitive system remains to be tested; the $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$ source term of path two is not given, so "$\chi>\chi_{\mathrm{c}}$ leads to deviation amplification" is not an inference of this model. Third, **fivefold recursion**: $I, V, T, E, D$ evolve in coupling, **with $\max V$ as the framework's meta-postulate** (the meta-postulate is the premise, the recursion is the mechanism, and the two do not stand in a "convergence" relation); this volume adopts a linearized conceptual framework under fast–slow time-scale separation, and the fully bidirectionally coupled ODE/PDE system is an open problem. Fourth, **a family of falsifiable predictions**: covering early warning of horizon collapse in reinforcement learning, failure determination for the structural identity criterion, and so on.

SRVD takes the Landauer principle, the second law of thermodynamics and the like as interfaces to external physical constraints; cross-scale dynamics does not thereby automatically gain experimental verification, and the relevant constraints can be applied to a concrete model only when the variable mapping, system boundary and applicability conditions are all satisfied. The framework can serve as a candidate macroscopic language for research on AI alignment, civilizational governance and the evolution of complexity.

**Evidence-level convention.** This volume labels every formal statement with one of four level tags: **Theorem**: premises are fully listed, the derivation closes, and the conclusion holds strictly under those premises; **Proposition**: has a formal statement, but the premises are in part to be calibrated, or some step of the derivation depends on an unproven modeling assumption; **Candidate Corollary**: derived from some proposition under additional conditions and not yet independently tested; **Hypothesis**: an empirical or mechanistic conjecture with no formal derivation. Wherever a statement is labeled **Proposition**, the main text also gives three lines — premises, result and failure conditions. This volume calls no conclusion that depends on an uncalibrated parameter a theorem.

**Keywords**: viability potential; decoder relativity; persistent structure; information–time–energy; Landauer principle; dynamical systems; falsifiability

**Overview of the framework propositions.** The eleven below are this volume's framework propositions: each has a formal statement and a discussable conclusion, but at least one of the parameters, thresholds or functional form is not yet calibrated, so all are labeled **Proposition**. Each can be refuted on its own, with the refutation condition listed in the right column; the corresponding sections of the main text give the full premises, derivation and domain of validity.

| No. | Framework proposition | Refutation condition (summary) |
| :---- | :------------------------- | :---------------------------- |
| 1 | $\max V$ is the long-term macroscopic goal of negentropic structures; what the active phase actually executes is $V^{\mathrm{val}}$ | The direction of strategy drift is unrelated to the ordering of $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$, or the rate has no monotonic relation to their difference |
| 2 | The low-order coarse-grained baseline $V \approx I\cdot T/E$ and its power-law generalization | Rank correlation is not significant, or after in-domain calibration the residuals show systematic structure |
| 3 | Four classes of substrate and three orders of cognition | A structure can be neither classified nor labeled as mixed, or the three-order groupings contradict one another |
| 4 | Passive–active–cognitive continuum; $V_{\mathrm{obj}}=V_{\mathrm{s}}+V_{\mathrm{c}}$ as a conditional model aggregation | An operational proxy of $V_{\mathrm{c}}$ is stably measured as zero rather than as a very small positive number |
| 5 | Decoder primacy: $D \to I \to V$ | There exists an information quantity that can be stably measured, and can independently predict $V$, without specifying a decoder |
| 6 | The elasticity exponents are log–log slopes and dimensionless; the ordering is a hypothesis | After a pre-registered test is passed, repeated measurement in the same domain stably gives $\alpha/\gamma \le 1$ |
| 7 | The endogenous time horizon is bounded by $T_{\mathrm{min}}<T_{\mathrm{pred}}\le\min(T_{\mathrm{struct}}^{\max},T_{\mathrm{env}})$ | Adding $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ improves no predictive power |
| 8 | Triple splitting and the two paths after the splitting | $V^{\mathrm{val}}$ can be fully explained by the current value of $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ |
| 9 | Three-way partition of information polarity, $I^{+}/I^{-}/I^{0}$ | The same input appears as both $I^{+}$ and $I^{-}$ and cannot be split by time period or scale |
| 10 | Fivefold recursion $I\to V\to T\to E\to D$ | If adding reverse coupling changes the qualitative behavior, the one-way chain is retained only as a local approximation |
| 11 | Three-resource screening: $I,T,E$ are mutually irreplaceable, and $D$ is not a fourth class of resource | A fourth independent resource dimension must be introduced to restore predictive power |

## Chapter 1 — The Ontological Layer: Persistent Structures and Decoders

This chapter fixes the framework's minimal ontology: what counts as a persistent structure, who is qualified to read it as a decoder, and the physical background in which the two sit. All variables from here on are defined on these three things.

### 1.1 Persistent Structures

**Definition: A persistent structure (PS) is an ordered negentropic configuration that forms under energy constraints, can be distinguished from its background, and can maintain its identifiable topological organization within a finite time window.** Here "topological organization" means the organizational form of its key relations, and does not require the material substrate to remain unchanged. It holds when three conditions are met: there is an ordered negentropic organization distinguishable from the background; the organization can keep its continuity within a finite time window; and sufficient energy conditions exist to support its persistence.

"Finite" means non-infinite: every persistent structure is subject to the ultimate thermodynamic boundary, the boundary of cosmic evolution, or limits set by the physical constants, and this does not mean a narrow time scale. Granite can persist on the order of a billion years and is still finite in time; "finite" and "persistent" do not conflict. So whether a structure is "persistent" does not depend on the length of its absolute lifetime, but on whether, under given energy conditions, it can keep its own topological features identifiable within the corresponding time window. Quantum states, crystals, stars, genomes, neural networks, language, institutions and civilizations differ enormously in scale, composition and medium, but in the dynamical sense belong to one class of object: ordered and distinguishable topological configurations formed and maintained under energy constraints. The framework does not presuppose an ontological rupture between the "physical" and the "abstract"; all later core variables are defined on persistent structures and are distinguished by their substrate, maintenance cost and manner of causal execution.

#### 1.1.1 Substrate Layering

| Substrate type | Dominant energy term | Role of $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | Core of persistence | Typical instances |
| :--------- | :----------- | :---------- | :---------- | :---------------- |
| **Barrier-frozen** | $E_{\mathrm{barrier}}$ | Almost no active causal invocation | The structure does not disperse | Crystals, rocks; corpses, paper books and powered-off hard disks, whose organization is maintained within the specified window mainly by structural constraint |
| **Physically dissipative** | $E_{\mathrm{survival}}$ | No cognition; continuous physical dissipation | Disperses once energy is cut | Stars, flames, typhoons, ocean currents, Bénard convection |
| **Actively dissipative: embodied type** | $E_{\mathrm{survival}} + E_{\mathrm{barrier}} + E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ (causal-processing cost usually low) | Low-power biochemical integration, physical probing | Metabolism plus genetic template | Plants, fungi, bacteria, lower animals such as tardigrades |
| **Actively dissipative: online type** | $E_{\mathrm{survival}} + E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | Associative learning and online simulation | Metabolism plus neural cognition | Crows, dogs, most animals |
| **Actively dissipative: symbolic type** | $E_{\mathrm{survival}} + E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | Offline multi-path counterfactual deduction | Metabolism plus symbolic cognition | Humans, embodied AI |
| **Externally substrate-dependent: viral type** | Energy consumption of the host's replication machinery (paid by the host) | No causal invocation of its own; replicates by borrowing host machinery | Continued availability of the host's replication machinery | Viral lineages |
| **Externally substrate-dependent: formal-mathematical type** | When invoked externally, the corresponding $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ is paid by the bearer | Formal deduction and relational invocation | Mathematical/logical relations are correctly instantiated or invoked | Mathematical structures, logical relations, geometric structures |
| **Externally substrate-dependent: memetic-virtual type** | $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ (paid by the host) | Propagation and diffusion | Maintenance of consensus | Money, religion, language |
| **Externally substrate-dependent: software type** | Energy consumption of the running hardware (paid by the runtime environment) | Executed by computing hardware | Code, parameters and stored state are preserved and run | Software, model parameters |

Within the actively dissipative types, the subdivision follows the position on the three $D_{\mathrm{cog}}$ axes: the embodied region (all three axes low), the online region (three axes medium to high), the symbolic region (three axes high); the online region is the lower transition band of the symbolic region. Within the framework there is no "purely abstract" structure with zero physical maintenance cost: an externally substrate-dependent structure always has $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ or running energy paid by an external bearing system.

**Delimitation of the externally substrate-dependent type.** An external substrate includes not only material storage media but also external systems that provide computational realization, biological replication, cognitive invocation, social propagation or institutional maintenance. Ordinary energy needs, metabolic dependence, niche dependence or general environmental influence do not by themselves constitute the criterion; the key is whether the external system bears a function necessary for the characteristic informational organization to be realized, replicated or continued, and that function cannot be equivalently replaced by the PS's own internal mechanisms. Points to handle for the four subtypes: the viral type takes the replicative continuation of the lineage as the object of analysis, with the physical persistence of a single particle analyzed separately; in the memetic-virtual type, preservation of a symbolic record does not necessarily mean that the corresponding structure is still effective, nor does the disappearance of a single substrate necessarily mean that the structure cannot continue; the formal-mathematical type must distinguish the structure itself from its expressive substrate: mathematical, logical and geometric relations belong to this type; physical laws, as objective physical relations, are taken into the physical channel and do not depend on human formulation for their existence. Symbols, formulas, proof texts, textbooks and communication protocols are representations or records of these structures, and are not automatically identical to a mathematical structure merely because they carry mathematical content. When a formal mathematical structure is invoked, the external cognitive or computational system bears the corresponding energy consumption; everyday mathematical reasoning may also be merely cognitive activity internal to the subject. **The mathematical-form persistent structures referred to in this volume include logical relations, mathematical relations and physical laws.** Of these, physical laws, as objective structural relations, are assigned to the physical channel and do not depend on human formulation for their existence; the concrete written records of proofs, symbols and formulas, textbooks and communication protocols are expressive, recording or propagating substrates, and are not automatically identical to the mathematical structure itself merely because they carry mathematical content. The software type must distinguish code, parameters, stored state and a given running instance; the stopping of one instance does not necessarily mean that the software structure has vanished.

**Relation to the decoder.** The classification describes the main conditions of persistence and does not directly decide whether $D$ exists or what type it is. That an external system supplies the conditions of bearing does not mean that all of its functions belong to the target PS's own decoder; decoder qualification is still judged independently by criteria. **Measurement boundary**: a complex object may contain substructures of different levels; a mixed representation, in which the same object is assigned differently under different boundaries or time windows, does not constitute a fifth type; when measuring $I$, $T$, $E$, one must first fix the target PS's boundary, scale and time window; the information, time and resource cost of an external bearing system must not be counted directly without justification; where a substrate is shared or several PSs run together, a cost-allocation rule must be declared first, to avoid double counting.

**Merging into three orders of cognition.** Across the whole framework, the cognitive hierarchy is merged uniformly into three orders, with no further orders added through the subdivision of substrates:

1. **First order: strictly zero cognition ($D = \varnothing$).** The barrier-frozen and physically dissipative types, such as rocks, crystals, flames, typhoons, thermostats. Features: $V_{\mathrm{c}} \equiv 0$, $T_{\mathrm{pred}} \equiv 0$, no decoder of its own; its structural information can be read by an external reading protocol (such as $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$).
2. **Second order: minimal living cognition (embodied region).** Plants, fungi, bacteria. Features: $V_{\mathrm{c}} \to 0^{+}$, all three axes low, time scale of plasticity leaning toward the transgenerational.
3. **Third order: higher-order symbolic cognition (online region and above).** Humans, embodied AI; the online-type cognition of crows, octopuses and the like falls into this order as its lower transition band and is not listed as a separate level. Features: $V_{\mathrm{c}} \gg 0$, three axes medium-high to high.

![Figure Ⅰ-2. Substrate Taxonomy of Persistent Structures](figures/release/fig_i2_carrier_taxonomy.pdf)

**Figure Ⅰ-2. Substrate Taxonomy of Persistent Structures.** The four top-level classes (barrier-frozen, physically dissipative, actively dissipative, externally substrate-dependent) with their dominant maintenance mechanisms, typical instances, and decoder positions. The cognitive position (embodied / online / symbolic regions) is a continuous region within the actively dissipative class, not a classification axis parallel to substrate type. Conceptual figure, no numerical values.

### 1.2 The Decoder

#### 1.2.1 The Definition of the Decoder

**Without a decoder, there is no information (as effective causal information).** A public reading protocol (such as $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$) can read out structural information, but it is not a decoder holder and produces no effective causal information. The decoder ($D$) is the physical mechanism by which a persistent structure extracts effective causal information from its latent configuration in order to maintain its own persistence; it is the interface between the structure and the physical channel, and determines how energy flows in, is converted and is allocated.

From an operationalization standpoint, the essence of the decoding process is **physical-state coupling and information mapping**: $D$ extracts, with efficiency $\eta_D \in [0,1]$, the currently activated subset $I_{\mathrm{active}} \equiv \eta_D \cdot I_{\mathrm{latent}}$ from the latent information $I_{\mathrm{latent}}$. Low-efficiency decoding ($\eta_D \to 0$) is common in passive structures, while highly efficient recursive decoding is a higher-order feature of the active phase. Net effective causal information $I_{\mathrm{net}} = \varphi_{\mathrm{s}} I_{\mathrm{struct}} + \varphi_{\mathrm{c}} I_{\mathrm{causal}}$ is a coarse-grained baseline quantity composed from the two channels with energy-allocation weights. $I_{\mathrm{active}}$ is defined from $I_{\mathrm{latent}}$, and $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ is a weighted composition of $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ and $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$; the current definitions do not imply any general containment relation between the two, or an upper bound $I_{\mathrm{active}} \le I_{\mathrm{net}}$, and this volume does not presuppose that relation as an established conclusion.

The decoder is itself a persistent structure and likewise obeys viability dynamics: $V(D) \approx I_{\mathrm{net}}(D)\cdot T(D)/E_{\mathrm{eff}}(D)$ (low-order approximation, with $T$ taken as the structural persistence time), so the observer and the observed form a self-consistent closed loop. The dependence chain is **$D \to I \to V$** (for a passive structure without a decoder, $D$ is taken to be the public reading protocol): any theory that discusses "information processing", "information flow" or "informational value" without first defining its decoder is regarded, within the SRVD framework, as having an incomplete definition.

#### 1.2.2 The Physical Decoder $D_{\mathrm{phys}}$

The physical decoder is a selective extraction mechanism **with a self-maintained boundary**: it possesses a self-maintaining boundary, however crude, and its selective extraction of environmental differences serves the persistence of the very structure behind that boundary. Internally it divides further into two layers: **static** — the selection rules are fixed by a long historical process and are not rewritten by environmental details during operation, as when an ion channel opens only to sodium ions, or a tardigrade triggers dormancy once dehydrated to a certain degree; **dynamic** — the structure itself has real-time feedback, compares "now" with "a moment ago" and adjusts its response accordingly, as in bacterial chemotaxis and plant phototropism.

**Structures without a self-maintained boundary are not included.** A stone, a crystal, a star has no "self" being guarded and falls under $D = \varnothing$. A thermostat also does not belong to $D_{\mathrm{phys}}$: what it maintains is the comfort of the people in the room, not its own boundary, and its effect is counted under the user's name. Having feedback, a threshold and a state switch is not enough to bring a structure automatically into $D_{\mathrm{phys}}$; the key is whether this selective extraction is maintaining the extractor's own boundary. The embodied-type cognitive decoder (the biochemical integration and physical probing of plants, fungi and bacteria) is based on $D_{\mathrm{phys}}$ and does not constitute a separate level.

#### 1.2.3 The Cognitive Decoder $D_{\mathrm{cog}}$

The cognitive decoder is a **continuous spectrum**, not a binary switch, characterized by three continuous axes: **degree of history dependence** (how much of the internal state is determined by past experience rather than entirely by the current input), **degree of persistence coupling** (how tightly this history dependence is bound to the structure's own persistence accounting), and **time scale of plasticity** (whether plasticity is welded in across generations or can be rewritten within an individual's lifetime). Roughly, the three axes mark out three regions:

| Region | Position on the three axes | Typical instances |
| :--------- | :------------------------- | :----------------------- |
| Embodied region | All three axes low; time scale of plasticity leaning transgenerational | Bacterial adaptive regulation, habituation in the sensitive plant, plant tropisms |
| Online region | Three axes medium to high; time scale of plasticity leaning toward an individual's lifetime | Crows, octopuses, most mammals |
| Symbolic region | Three axes high; offline deduction, counterfactuals and symbol manipulation appear | Humans, some AI |

The concrete measurement protocol for the three axes has yet to be established, and the fourth axis, "degree of self-reference", currently has only a conceptual definition. The three regions are a coarse-grained statement of the three-axis space and correspond one-to-one to the merging into three orders; systems such as thermostats, rocks and flames have only $D = \varnothing$ and do not enter the $D_{\mathrm{cog}}$ spectrum at all.

#### 1.2.4 The Activity Criterion for Decoders

A system is an "actively persisting" decoder if and only if it satisfies three minimal conditions at once: **controllable internal state** (it can change its own behavior according to an internal model, rather than merely being driven from outside); **adjustable energy flow** (it can actively raise or lower $E_{\mathrm{flow}}$ in response to environmental change); **predictable time horizon** ($T_{\mathrm{pred}} > 0$, and it can regulate current behavior accordingly).

These three are the **active-phase criteria**; they judge only the property "active persistence" and do not replace the **four general qualification conditions** of the decoder. The four conditions are those of Volume IV §2.2, Definition 2.2, word for word the same, none omissible: ① **self-maintaining an identifiable boundary**; ② **selectively extracting environmental differences**; ③ **state updating triggered by the extraction**; ④ **that update bearing causally on its own persistence**. Only a system that first satisfies all four goes on to receive this section's activity classification. Accordingly: rocks, planets, stars, flames and thermostats do not satisfy all the conditions and fall under $D = \varnothing$; bacteria, plants and fungi satisfy the activity conditions and fall under $D_{\mathrm{phys}}$; crows, octopuses, mammals, humans and embodied AI satisfy the activity conditions and have a three-axis position at the online type or above, and fall under $D_{\mathrm{phys}} + D_{\mathrm{cog}}$. The object of judgment for the criteria is the system as a whole (a cell, an individual, a robot); static selection rules (ion channels, dormancy switches) are internal components of the holder, do not separately undergo this criterion, and their attribution follows the holder.

> **Classification of stars.** A star does have internal feedback (such as the negative feedback between core temperature and fusion rate), but it does not rely on an internal predictive model and has no self-maintained boundary, so $D=\varnothing$: its effective horizon equals $T_{\mathrm{struct}}^{\max}$ fixed by its fuel reserve, and cannot be actively lengthened or compressed by internal state. That is the reason it is passively persisting, and not "whether energy-flow regulation exists".

#### 1.2.5 Summary of Decoder Symbols

| Symbol | Name | Definition | Scope of application |
| :---- | :----------- | :------------------------- | :---------------- |
| $D = \varnothing$ | No decoder | A purely physical structure without a self-maintained boundary | Rocks, crystals, stars, flames, thermostats |
| $D_{\mathrm{phys}}$ | Physical decoder | Selective extraction with a self-maintained boundary, divided into static and dynamic | Bacteria, plants, fungi, lower animals |
| $D_{\mathrm{cog}}$ | Cognitive decoder | Functional complexity superimposed on $D_{\mathrm{phys}}$, a three-axis continuous space | Crows, octopuses, mammals, humans, embodied AI |
| $\eta_D$ | Decoding efficiency | $\eta_D \in [0,1]$, $I_{\mathrm{active}} \equiv \eta_D \cdot I_{\mathrm{latent}}$ | All PSs |
| $\mathcal{T}$ | Topological transition operator | The operation that opens a new causal dimension when a topological transition occurs | Active phase only |
| $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$ | Rule depth (static definition) | The minimum number of independent rules the decoder $D$ needs to reconstruct $P$ within tolerance (0 when there is no rule shorter than point-by-point recording) | All PSs; 1 for a perfect crystal |
| $D_{\mathrm{depth}}^{\mathrm{dyn}}$ | Depth exponent (dynamical definition) | $1 + \#\{\mathcal{T}\}$, i.e. the transition count plus one; defined only for objects whose static depth is at least 1 | Active phase with a transition history; always 1 in the passive phase |

**The two-layer meaning of $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$.** The two things must be kept apart, or a calibration assumption will be read as a mathematical equality.

**The static layer is a definition**: $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$ is the minimum number of independent rules the decoder $D$ needs to reconstruct an object to within a specified tolerance; for a completely random object, where no rule shorter than point-by-point recording can be found, it takes the value 0. A perfect crystal has only one rule ("all white"), $D_{\mathrm{depth}} = 1$; a watch has several independent rules — gear transmission, spring energy storage, hand indication — and a higher depth; a snowflake dendrite is the same rule repeated at different scales, and repeated unfolding does not add depth. This is this volume's formal definition of "rule depth", and the only wording used as a definition.

**The dynamical layer is the depth exponent**: taking the number of transitions as an evolutionary proxy for depth, it is written $D_{\mathrm{depth}}^{\mathrm{dyn}} \equiv 1 + \#\{\mathcal{T}\}$, where $\#\{\mathcal{T}\}$ is the number of topological transitions up to the time of observation. A quantity carrying the superscript $\mathrm{dyn}$ is not an "actual number of rules"; it shares the same intuition as the static definition (more rules, deeper), and the two values need not be equal.

The two cannot be treated as one quantity: a single transition may introduce several independent rules at once (from Newtonian mechanics to relativity, one transition rewrites three groups of relations — spacetime, mass and energy — together), or may merely change how existing rules are organized without adding a rule (the same set of equations in an equivalent restatement). Therefore "each transition adds exactly one independent rule" is a **calibration assumption**, not deduced from the static definition. In writing $D_{\mathrm{depth}}^{\mathrm{dyn}} = 1 + \#\{\mathcal{T}\}$, this volume declares that assumption at the same time and, in the calibration method below, gives a way to test it; when the assumption fails the depth exponent can still be used, but it is then only a transition count, no longer a depth estimate.

**Calibration method.** Take a set of comparable objects (same domain, same observation scale, same tolerance), independently measure the static depth $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$ for each (by the minimum code length or the minimum rule-set count within tolerance), and at the same time count the number of transitions $\#\{\mathcal{T}\}$ from the historical record, and run the regression

$$
D_{\mathrm{depth}} = a + b\cdot \#\{\mathcal{T}\} + \varepsilon_{\mathrm{res}} \tag{I-1.1}
$$

The parent proposition $D_{\mathrm{depth}}=1+\#\{\mathcal{T}\}$ **predicts $a=1$ and $b=1$ at the same time**, so both must be tested together: equivalence tolerances for the intercept and the slope are set in advance, and an equivalence test (for example TOST) is carried out on each; an ordinary difference test that merely fails to reject $a=1$ or $b=1$ is not enough to show that either equals 1. The residuals must also show no trend that varies systematically with object type (a homogeneity test). Testing only $b$ would weaken falsifiability — if the author holds that $a$ is affected by the measurement zero point and should not be imposed, the reason must be stated here, and the $a=1$ of Volume II §2.4 marked "optional". When both pass, "each transition adds one independent rule" can serve as a working hypothesis within that domain; a slope significantly above 1 indicates that one transition introduces several rules on average, and the depth exponent should be rewritten as $1 + b\cdot\#\{\mathcal{T}\}$ and recalibrated; a slope significantly below 1 or near 0 indicates that transitions mainly change the organization of the rules rather than their number, in which case the depth exponent can serve only as a proxy for "the number of paradigm changes" and cannot be used for depth comparison. The regression's tolerance, coding scheme and transition-counting rule must be locked before the test; post hoc tuning does not count as passing.

**Note on notation**: the $D$ in $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$ is taken from depth, and is not a decoder-attribution symbol.

### 1.3 The Physical Channel

**Definition**: The physical channel ($C_{\mathrm{phys}}$) is the environmental foundation in which a persistent structure and its decoder sit; it comprises the fundamental interactions and all the underlying rules that emerge from them; within the known framework of physics, it supplies the conduits for energy flow and the protocols for informational interaction. The operation of any decoder is subject to three nested constraint layers:

- **Underlying-law constraint**: the electromagnetic, gravitational, strong and weak nuclear forces supply the underlying constraints on energy transfer and signal transmission, and are the physical base that no decoder can overstep.
- **Medium-structure constraint**: the mesoscopic media (air, water, vacuum, nerve fibers) formed by the aggregation of fundamental interactions in particular regions of spacetime impose bandwidth limits, delay and attenuation on signals, and determine the amount of effective signal a decoder can extract.
- **Thermodynamic-lower-bound constraint**: thermal fluctuations and dissipation introduce channel noise; only after a testable mapping has been established between logically irreversible erasure and SRVD's information variables does the Landauer principle supply a conditional energy lower bound for the corresponding $E_{\mathrm{compute}}$, and it does not directly give a lower bound for the general $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ or for an arbitrary quantity of effective causal information.

In this framework $C_{\mathrm{phys}}$ is treated as a fixed background and not as a dynamical variable; it determines the microscopic barrier height $\Delta U_{\mathrm{barrier}}$, the efficiency limit of $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$, and the maximum possible value of $I_{\mathrm{net}}$. It is the constraint background to which all decoders are jointly subject: the second law of thermodynamics and the speed-of-light causal limit have a universal objectivity across cultures and species precisely because any decoder that violates them incurs an irreversible practical penalty. This is the fundamental criterion that distinguishes $C_{\mathrm{phys}}$ from cultural consensus.

![Figure Ⅰ-3. The Minimal Theoretical Architecture of SRVD](figures/release/fig_i3_minimal_architecture.pdf)

**Figure Ⅰ-3. The Minimal Theoretical Architecture of SRVD.** The decoder $D$ reads the identifiable organization of the persistent structure PS, yielding the triple readings $I$, $T$, and $E$, which form the viability-potential reading $V$ under compatible pairing ($D \to (I,T,E) \to V$). The physical channel $C_{\mathrm{phys}}$ (underlying laws, medium structure, thermodynamic lower bounds) is the constraint background to which all decoders are jointly subject. The figure is a conceptual architecture, not a complete dynamical loop. Conceptual figure, no numerical values.

## Chapter 2 — The Variable Constitution

This chapter defines the core variables that run through the whole volume, in five families — information, energy, time, viability potential, decoder — plus the elasticity exponents and a few dynamical parameters. Each symbol receives one full definition here (with unit and domain of validity), and later uses only restate it in one sentence.

### 2.1 The $I$ Family (the Information Family)

**Terminology convention.** When "information" is used bare, it refers to the superordinate concept and not to a single measurable. The measurable information variables are structural information $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ and causal information $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$; the bare symbol $I$ in formulas defaults to $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ (unless explicitly declared otherwise), which is a model quantity composed from the two, not a directly measured quantity. Whenever "effective information" is discussed, it must always be written in full as "net effective causal information" (the composite quantity $I_{\mathrm{net}}$) or "effective causal information" ($I_{\mathrm{causal}}$ satisfying the three entry conditions for causal effects).

**Time convention.** The $I\cdot T$ in the viability-potential formula equals $\int I\,\mathrm{d}t$ only when $I$ is constant within $T$. This volume handles the point as follows: wherever $I$ is multiplied by $T$, $I$ always means the **mean reading within $T$**, $\bar I$, that is, $\bar I:=\frac{1}{T}\int_{S}I(t)\,\mathrm{d}t$, where $T=\mu(S)$ and $S$ is the set of times over which the reading holds (for the structural channel, the identifiable period; for the causal channel, the period in which the effect is identifiable). When $I$ is constant within the window, $\bar I=I$ and the original formula is unchanged; when $T=0$, $\bar I$ is undefined and no zero-over-zero occurs. A reported reading must state three things at once: the sampling time point or window, whether a mean or a single point is taken, and whether background drift was detected within the window. When there is only a single-point reading, it is marked "$I(t_0)$ single-point reading", and $V$ then serves only as an upper bound for a monotonically decaying $I$. Comparisons across objects always fix the same window.

**Two channels.** The low-order core equation takes $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ as input; fine-grained accounting uses the two-channel form, with inputs $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ and $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$:

$$
I_{\mathrm{net}} = \varphi_{\mathrm{s}} I_{\mathrm{struct}} + \varphi_{\mathrm{c}} I_{\mathrm{causal}}, \qquad \varphi_{\mathrm{s}} = \frac{E_{\mathrm{struct}}}{E_{\mathrm{eff}}}, \quad \varphi_{\mathrm{c}} = \frac{E_{\mathrm{causal}}}{E_{\mathrm{eff}}}, \quad \varphi_{\mathrm{s}} + \varphi_{\mathrm{c}} = 1 \tag{I-2.1}
$$

The channel weights are taken directly as the ratios of energy readings and are not parameters to be fitted; taking the weights as energy ratios is a modeling convention and not an independent derivation of $I$ from $E$, and the accounting scope of $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ (including the holder) must be declared before the weights. A purely structural type degenerates to $I_{\mathrm{net}}\approx I_{\mathrm{struct}}$, and a purely cognitive type to $I_{\mathrm{net}}\approx I_{\mathrm{causal}}$. When the two kinds of bit are weighted in this way without in-domain calibration, $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ serves only as an order-of-magnitude indicator and does not replace the readings of the two sub-channels.

**Additivity and numerical comparability are modeling conventions, not conclusions guaranteed automatically by units.** $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ and $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$ use different reading protocols, backgrounds and controls; that both are counted in bit is not enough to show that they sit on the same semantic scale. The energy shares $\varphi_{\mathrm{s}}$ and $\varphi_{\mathrm{c}}$ supply only an accountable conversion weight and do not prove that the two kinds of bit are interchangeable. Hence $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ is a coarse-grained composite index, not a directly measured quantity, and not a quantity derived from first principles of thermodynamics. Its value can be used quantitatively only when four conditions are met at once: (i) the two channels use the **same reading protocol** (the same measure, the same resolution, the same extreme direction); (ii) the **same background** and the same rule for constructing the control; (iii) the **same observation window**, with comparable sampling density for the two channels within the window; (iv) **in-domain calibration** has been completed, that is, on the same set of objects the weighted sum has been verified to stand in a monotonic and stable relation to some independent observable (survival time, output, resilience and so on). If any of the four is missing, $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ serves only as an **ordinal indicator**: one may say "A's $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ is higher than B's", but not "A is 1.7 times B", and still less compare its value across domains. Every weighted sum implies a conversion rate, and the conversion rate itself must have independent empirical grounds; in an uncalibrated domain the absolute value of a weighted sum has no meaning, and only its sign and order do.

**Measurement contract.** Any information reading must declare five operational commitments: the object of the reading, the background or control, the measure, the resolution and extreme direction, and the observation window and uncertainty. After any one of them is changed, the reading must be recalibrated and not compared directly with the old reading. Information holds only relative to a reader (a decoder and its holder, or a public reading protocol); after the object, holder or control is changed, the reading must not be carried over. For brevity, below, where no ambiguity arises, $I_{\mathrm{struct},D}(P)$ and $I_{\mathrm{causal},D}(P)$ are abbreviated to $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ and $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$, with $P$ and $D$ defaulting to the structure under discussion and its holder; when comparing different objects or holders the full notation is restored.

**Structural information $I_{\mathrm{struct},D}(P)$.** The default measure is rarity relative to the background: for a discrete reading $r=D(P)$, take $-\log_2\Pr_B(r)$; for an ordered reading, take the tail probability in a direction declared in advance; for a distributional reading, take relative entropy and state the estimation and smoothing. The background defaults to the natural frequency distribution of that reading in the holder's habitat; for a passive structure with no holder, a public reading protocol specifies the reference population; where none can be constructed, it must be stated explicitly as a modeler-specified background. Description length, effective dimension, configurational entropy and similar measures can replace the default measure, but must likewise declare the five operational commitments.

**Causal information $I_{\mathrm{causal},D}(P)$.** It measures how rare the observable effect produced by $P$ is relative to a control $P_0$. First declare $D$, $P$, $P_0$, the effect reading $e$ and the observation window, then measure the effect distribution $B_0$ under $P_0$:

$$
I_{\mathrm{causal},D}(P)=-\log_2\Pr_{B_0}\big(s(e')\ge s(e_{\mathrm{obs}})\big)
$$

$s$ is an effect statistic declared in advance; where smaller values are more extreme, $\le$ is used in reverse; for a discrete effect one may take $-\log_2\Pr_{B_0}(e=e_{\mathrm{obs}})$. An effect must satisfy three entry conditions at once — counterfactual difference, observability, and a measurable cost paid by the holder; a rare reading without an observable causal effect is not counted.

**Conditions for substitution into this volume's equations.** Information variables must satisfy: nonnegativity; still independently predicting changes in $V$ after $T$ and $E$ are controlled (causal efficacy; this is a test requirement on the model's explanatory power, not a definition of $I$); and not being reducible to a function of $T$ and $E$ (otherwise the product form degenerates into redundancy). Physical bounds apply only to quantities satisfying the corresponding definitions: the Landauer lower bound constrains the amount of logically irreversible erasure, and the Bekenstein bound (Bekenstein, 1981) and the holographic bound (Bousso, 2002) constrain physical entropy or the number of distinguishable states; substitution is allowed only once a mapping to those quantities has been established.

| Symbol | Unit | Definition | Scope of application |
| :----- | :---- | :--------------------------------- | :-------------- |
| $I$ | bit | Generalized structural difference: the total amount of distinguishable ordered difference of a persistent structure relative to its background | All PSs |
| $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ | bit | Structural information: the information content of the structural channel as defined by the measurement contract | All PSs |
| $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | bit | Causal information: the rarity of the effects that $P$ produces relative to the control baseline of the holder $D$; the effects must satisfy the three conditions of counterfactual difference, observability and measurable cost | When qualifying effects and a holder exist |
| $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ | bit | Net effective causal information: $\varphi_{\mathrm{s}} I_{\mathrm{struct}} + \varphi_{\mathrm{c}} I_{\mathrm{causal}}$; additivity is a modeling convention, and numerical use requires all four conditions; a composite model quantity, not a directly measured one | All PSs |
| $I_{\mathrm{latent}}$ | bit | Latent causal information: the stock of causal capacity that is not currently activated but recoverable | Active phase only |
| $I_{\mathrm{active}}$ | bit | Active causal information: $\eta_D \cdot I_{\mathrm{latent}}$ | Active phase only |
| $I_{\mathrm{max}}(D)$ | bit | The maximum information capacity that the decoder $D$ can extract | All PSs |
| $I_{\mathrm{min}}$ | bit | The minimum amount of information required to maintain identity | All PSs |
| $I^{+}$ / $I^{-}$ / $I^{0}$ | bit | Positive-effect / negative-effect / neutral: inputs that expand / compress the future reachable state space, or physical causal inputs with no persistence polarity | $I^{+}$ and $I^{-}$ in the active phase only |
| $\tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ | bit | Candidate paradigm-breaking signal: the rarity of $D_n$'s prediction residual on the target relative to its historical residual distribution, $-\log_2\Pr_{B_n}(\varepsilon_{\mathrm{res}}\ge\varepsilon_{\mathrm{obs}})$; the only gating variable before a transition, an observable proxy | Active phase only |
| $I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ | bit | Validated added information: the added effective causal information recognized after a transition has succeeded and been independently reproduced; an ex post quantity, not used as an ex ante gate | Active phase only |
| $I_{\mathrm{interp}}$ | bit | Paradigm-interpretive information: optimization within the existing paradigm, with rapidly decaying marginal efficacy | Active phase only |
| $I_{\mathrm{type}}$ / $I_{\mathrm{token}}$ | bit | Type information (an inheritable template) / token information (an instance) | All PSs |
| $I_{\mathrm{total}}$ | bit | A tag for the parallel record of structural information and causal information; until the semantic base and calibration are established, not regarded as a directly additive measured total | Conceptual quantity |

**Three-layer decomposition of paradigm-breaking information.** The table above separates three objects that were formerly written as one symbol. The three layers differ in temporal position, observability and use, and mixing them leads to circular argument.

**Layer one, the candidate paradigm-breaking signal $\tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}}$.** It is defined as the rarity of the existing decoder $D_n$'s prediction residual $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{res}}$ on the target relative to that decoder's historical residual distribution $B_n$: $\tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}}\equiv-\log_2\Pr_{B_n}(\varepsilon_{\mathrm{res}}\ge\varepsilon_{\mathrm{obs}})$. It is an **ex ante** quantity: it can be computed from $D_n$'s own historical behavior alone, with no need to know first whether the transition succeeds. Before use, four things must be done: pre-register the significance level $\alpha_0$ (for example $\alpha_0=10^{-3}$, corresponding to a threshold of $\tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}}\ge 9.97$ bit); pre-register how the residual distribution $B_n$ is constructed (sliding-window length, whether anomalous segments are removed, whether it is stratified); pre-register a **false-alarm rule** — the upper limit $N\alpha_0$ on the number of false alarms tolerated in $N$ independent gatings, with the requirement that $k$ consecutive threshold exceedances be needed before a candidate signal is declared ($k$ also pre-registered), to suppress false positives triggered by single noise events; and pre-register a **resolution condition** — the sample size $N_B$ of the reference distribution $B_n$ must satisfy $N_B\alpha_0\gtrsim10$ ($N_B\gtrsim10^4$ when $\alpha_0=10^{-3}$), otherwise the threshold is unresolvable (an empirical distribution caps the reportable reading at $\log_2N_B$, and at small counts there is a further bias of the same order), and one must switch to a parametric tail model, or lower $\alpha_0$ to the actually resolvable limit; each reading must be annotated with $N_B$ and the method of tail estimation. Only after these four are locked is $\tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ a reproducible gating reading; an unlocked rarity reading is merely a descriptive statistic.

**Layer two, the validated added information $I_{\mathrm{orig}}$.** It is recognized only after the transition has occurred and the new capability has been independently reproduced: it records "how much effective causal information, previously nonexistent and independently testable, this transition actually brought". This is an **ex post** quantity, used for bookkeeping, comparison and theory calibration, and it cannot in turn be used as an input condition for the transition.

**Layer three, the topological transition event $\mathcal{T}$.** This is the event itself and not an information quantity: a new causal dimension is opened, $I_{\mathrm{max}}(D_{n+1})>I_{\mathrm{max}}(D_n)$, and the depth exponent rises by one, as judged by comparing decoder states before and after the transition.

**Only layer one can enter the gate.** The reason is that only $\tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ is defined and computable before the transition. Gating on $I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ would form a circle (one must first know the transition succeeded in order to compute the signal), and gating on $\mathcal{T}$ amounts to predicting the result with the result. A restriction must also be stated: **a proxy reading above the threshold does not mean that a transition will necessarily occur**. A rare residual shows only that the existing decoder is markedly failing on that target; the failure may come from noise, distribution drift or measurement fault, or from a genuinely new dimension; the gate screens out only the candidate set "worth investing trial-and-error cost in", and whether a transition occurs still depends on subsequent construction, validation and resource conditions. Its purpose is to concentrate the limited $E_{\mathrm{build}}$ on a few candidates, not to announce that a breakthrough has arrived.

#### 2.1.1 Information Is a Relational Quantity, Not an Absolute One

In SRVD, $I$ is the amount of effective difference that a persistent structure can extract through its decoder $D$. The size of $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ depends not only on the latent information $I_{\mathrm{latent}}$ present in the environment but, more, on the structural features of $D$.

The same physical configuration presents completely different informational value to different decoders: oxygen is $I^{+}$ to humans and may be $I^{-}$ to anaerobic bacteria; visible electromagnetic waves are close to $I^{0}$ to a person blind from birth. Causal efficacy is a measure of the degree of match between a topological configuration and the structure of the decoder. From this follows an inference: under the current framework of cognition and measurement, the absolute values of $I$ across decoders cannot be compared directly — "how many bits does a stone have, how many bits does a person have" is not a meaningful same-domain question, unless a unified cross-decoder frame of reference is established in the future. A statement that "there is more information" must carry "under which decoder", or it is incomplete; the boundary of $I$ expands as the decoder is upgraded, opening information dimensions previously unreachable.

#### 2.1.2 Rules for Comparing the Viability Potential

Traditional information theory attempts to rank all structures in one-dimensional absolute order by $I = \log_2 \Omega$, which is in principle infeasible, because the state spaces of different systems do not lie in one coordinate system. This limitation is made precise as a rule of comparison for $V$.

**The viability potential is not a quantity that exists apart from a decoder.** Any value of $V$ is in essence $V_D(P)$: the viability potential of some persistent structure $P$ relative to some reader $D$. Where the main text does not specify a reference decoder, $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ means the viability potential relative to the macro reading protocol $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$, and is not an absolute theoretical quantity existing independently of all decoders. A comparison can be made only after the reference decoder is made explicit, and its legitimacy is jointly determined by the fixed states of the decoder, the measurement protocol and the environment.

| Type of comparison | Condition | Legitimacy | Semantics |
| :------- | :--------------------- | :---------- | :------------------- |
| $V_D(P_1)$ versus $V_D(P_2)$ | Same decoder, protocol, environment, observation window and analysis scale; consistent information definitions and energy attribution | Conditionally legitimate | The energy composition must also be comparable or independently calibrated, or no quantitative ranking is made |
| $V_{D_A}(P_A)$ versus $V_{D_B}(P_B)$ | $D_A \ne D_B$, no decoder mapping done | Undefined | Values from different measurement systems are not comparable |
| $V_D(P_A)$ versus $V_D(P_B)$ | Mapped to a common reference decoder $D$ | $V_{\mathrm{s}}$ legitimate; $V_{\mathrm{c}}$ undefined | $V_{\mathrm{s}}$ can be ranked under unified physical measurement; $V_{\mathrm{c}}$ awaits the establishment of a generalized $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$ |
| Comparison of $\mathrm{d}\ln V / \mathrm{d}t$ | The time parametrization and the definition of $V$ have consistent physical semantics | A scale-robust candidate indicator | Invariant under rescaling by a multiplicative constant; does not extend to nonlinear redefinition |

**The macro reading protocol $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$.** The idealized macroscopic reading protocol implicitly adopted by classical physics (SI units, isolated system, no measurement disturbance) can serve as a public measurement coordinate for some cross-subject comparisons, in two layers: **the narrow $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$** directly adopts classical-physics measurement rules and covers the measurement of $I_{\mathrm{struct}}, T_{\mathrm{struct}}, E_{\mathrm{struct}}$ in $V_{\mathrm{s}}$, but cross-subject quantitative comparison must also unify object boundaries, observation windows, the operational definition of information and the energy-attribution convention, and requires that the energy composition be comparable or already calibrated — a common unit and protocol are necessary conditions, not sufficient ones; **the generalized $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$** is a natural extension of the logic of classical measurement and attempts to cover $I_{\mathrm{causal}}, T_{\mathrm{causal}}, E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ in $V_{\mathrm{c}}$; these quantities all finally show up as physical effects and in theory admit measurement, but a unified operational protocol is currently lacking, and cross-subject comparison of $V_{\mathrm{c}}$ is therefore treated as "undefined".

$D_{\mathrm{macro}}$ is the reading protocol that the notation $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ itself takes as its default reference: a $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ apart from all decoders does not exist, and there is no objective quantity that stands independently prior to it, waiting to be "assigned". At the operational level, $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ can only approach its theoretical value under $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$ through concrete measurement; when measurement cost is very high, evaluation precision is constrained by $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$, and when the parties cannot reach consensus on key parameters, the effective $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ may fall far below the theoretical upper limit that holds when the physical substrate is intact. $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$ is an idealized protocol and not a persistent structure, and $V(D)$ does not apply to it; a concrete reading is always executed, and its cost borne, by some holder or measuring apparatus.

**Incommensurability across observation scales.** If two observation scales $S_A \ne S_B$ cause the system to adopt different coarse-graining cutoff frequencies or measurement protocols, then $\Delta V = V(\cdot\mid S_A)-V(\cdot\mid S_B)$ is undefined; this limitation does not disappear by introducing $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$, unless the protocol has explicitly unified the scales. Unmodeled errors can cancel only if the compared objects are subject to the same kind of multiplicative bias and the bias acts in the same way on each energy item; they do not cancel automatically.

**Rules for quantitative comparison.** (1) Within-domain quantitative: relative values may be compared only within one system type, with boundary / decoder / protocol / environment / observation window / scale / operational definition / cost attribution consistent, and with similar energy composition or independent calibration. (2) Cross-domain qualitative: across system types only qualitative statements are allowed (such as "both satisfy $\alpha/\gamma>1$"), and side-by-side numerical comparison of the three exponents is prohibited. (3) Conditional cross-subject comparison of $V_{\mathrm{s}}$: after fixing the narrow $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$ and the items above, the operational definition and the energy-attribution convention must also be consistent; a common unit across species is by itself insufficient to support direct ranking. (4) $V_{\mathrm{c}}$ is currently undefined and requires the generalized $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$. An absolute cross-subject ranking of $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ does not hold (see §10.5).

### 2.2 The $E$ Family (the Energy Family)

The $E$ family quantifies the various energy costs that a persistent structure pays in the course of persistence; the basic unit is $\mathrm{J}$.

| Symbol | Definition | Relations and boundaries |
| :---- | :-------------------------- | :-------------------------- |
| $E$ | Generalized energy, the parent variable of the energy family | — |
| $E_{\mathrm{barrier}}$ | Barrier-related amortized structural cost: the cost allocated to the target observation window by a declared rule, not equal to the barrier height | The barrier height is recorded separately as $\Delta U_{\mathrm{barrier}}$ |
| $E_{\mathrm{survival}}$ | The energy integral within the observation window spent on actively maintaining structural organization | Under the three-way decomposition a sub-item of $E_{\mathrm{struct}}$; must not be added again to the complete $E_{\mathrm{struct}}$; power is recorded separately as $\dot E_{\mathrm{survival}}$ |
| $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | The energy cost, within the observation window, attributed to the specified holder and spent on reading out or processing the target causal effect | Can be split into $E_{\mathrm{compute}}+E_{\mathrm{refresh}}$; does not automatically include all of the holder's metabolism, and is not double-counted with $E_{\mathrm{struct}}$ |
| $E_{\mathrm{struct}}$ | The energy cost, within the observation window, attributed to the structural channel and spent on maintaining the identifiable organization of the target PS | Under the three-way decomposition $E_{\mathrm{struct}}=E_{\mathrm{barrier}}+E_{\mathrm{survival}}$; the lower bound is recorded separately as $E_{\mathrm{struct}}^{\min}$ |
| $E_{\mathrm{flow}}$ | The cumulative activity energy budget within the observation window (J); "flow" means cumulative accounting and does not denote power | Baseline subtotal $E_{\mathrm{survival}}+E_{\mathrm{causal}}$; no longer added to $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ as an independent item; instantaneous power is recorded as $\dot E_{\mathrm{flow}}$ |
| $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ | The complete effective thermodynamic cost of maintaining the target PS and its declared causal channels, within the specified accounting boundary and observation window | Basic form $E_{\mathrm{struct}}+E_{\mathrm{causal}}$; expandable into a sum of three terms only when the window and scope are the same, the sub-items are mutually exclusive and coverage is complete |
| $E_{\mathrm{struct}}^{\min}$ | The minimum energy required to maintain the structural channel within the specified boundary and observation window | Compared only with $E_{\mathrm{struct}}$ on the same basis; not a lower bound on total cost, and not used to mark the per-bit erasure lower bound |
| $\mathcal{E}_D$ | The mean energy scale (J) of the decoder's physical system entering the upper bound on the logical operation rate | Not equal to the cumulative $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ or $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$; the system boundary must be declared first |
| $E_{\mathrm{available}}$ | The maximum free-energy budget the system can obtain within the declared boundary | Plays a different role from $E_{\mathrm{thresh}}$, and no size relation between them is presupposed; if the environmental supply ceiling needs to be distinguished, record $E_{\mathrm{max}}$ with $E_{\mathrm{available}}\le E_{\mathrm{max}}$ |
| $E_{\mathrm{floor}}$ | The lower bound on total effective cost: $E_{\mathrm{eff}}\ge E_{\mathrm{floor}}\ge E_{\mathrm{struct}}^{\min}>0$ | Differs from $E_{\mathrm{struct}}^{\min}$: the latter is only the lower bound of the structural channel |
| $E_{\mathrm{thresh}}$ | Damage threshold: the triggering condition of the irreversible damage term in the model | Directly comparable with $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ only when on the same boundary, the same observation window and compatible definitions; physical mechanism and calibration to be established |
| $E_{\mathrm{build}}$ | Construction cost: the one-time physical energy for creating a new structure or upgrading an existing one | Assessed separately by $L_{\mathrm{lev}}$, and does not enter the dynamical formulas of $V$ |
| $E_{\mathrm{compute}}$ | Energy consumed by computation (including erasure) | The Landauer lower bound applies only to measurable logically irreversible erasure |
| $E_{\mathrm{refresh}}$ | Energy consumed by maintenance (refresh, error correction, fidelity) | The corresponding lower bound applies only where measurable logically irreversible erasure is involved; a general refresh lower bound has not been established |
| $E_{\mathrm{decode}}$ | A functional label (provisional) for cognitive decoding activity | No independent, non-overlapping physical sub-item yet; used only as a functional annotation, not summed independently |
