**Construction cost** ($E_{\mathrm{build}}$) is the one-time physical energy paid to create a new structure or to upgrade an existing structure to a higher decoder level. It differs fundamentally in nature from maintenance dissipation: $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ is a continuing flow that enters the dynamical formulas of $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$; $E_{\mathrm{build}}$ is a historical stock that does not enter the dynamical equations, and its utility must be assessed separately.

A structure with high construction cost is at first favored by its stability, but is very easily locked into the old paradigm by the sunk-cost effect: it keeps consuming physical free energy but cannot generate new candidate paradigm-breaking signals ($\tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}}\to 0$, that is, the existing decoder no longer fails on its own targets, and so there is no reason to invest in reconstruction), forming a thermodynamically inert state. Construction cost enters persistence decisions through a **risk function**:

$$
R(E_{\mathrm{build}}) = 1 - \exp\left(-\frac{E_{\mathrm{build}}}{E_{\mathrm{topo}}}\right), \qquad \Pr(\text{lock-in})\ \text{increases monotonically with}\ R \tag{I-7.1}
$$

$E_{\mathrm{topo}}$ is the characteristic topological energy corresponding to the topological level at which the current decoder sits (written $E_{\mathrm{topo}}(D_n)$, varying with decoder level).

### 7.2 The Wisdom Lever Coefficient

Define the **wisdom lever coefficient**

$$
L_{\mathrm{lev}} = \frac{\Delta V}{E_{\mathrm{build}}} \tag{I-7.2}
$$

It quantifies the efficiency with which a small energy investment leverages a large jump in viability potential. A contrast of two extreme strategies:

| Strategy | Features | Typical value of $L_{\mathrm{lev}}$ |
| :----------------------- | :------------------------ | :---------- |
| **Paradigm-breaking type** ($\tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ crosses the threshold and is confirmed ex post by $I_{\mathrm{orig}}$) | A small construction cost triggers a topological transition of the decoder; $\Delta V$ is huge | $L_{\mathrm{lev}} \gg 1$ |
| **Pure resource-accumulation type** (arms races, bureaucratic expansion) | A large amount of $E_{\mathrm{build}}$ is invested with no paradigm shift; the marginal $\Delta V$ tends to zero | $L_{\mathrm{lev}} \to 0$ |

The "$\gg 1$" and "$\to 0$" in the table are both qualitative intervals, not numerical values comparable across domains. The denominator of $L_{\mathrm{lev}}$ is in joules and the numerator in $\mathrm{bit\cdot s/J}$, so its dimension is $\mathrm{bit\cdot s/J^2}$, and the $L_{\mathrm{lev}}$ of different systems cannot be compared directly in size; only between strategies of the same system, the same target and the same observation window is this ratio meaningful. The topological energy threshold of a paradigm breakthrough is

$$
E_{\mathrm{build}}^{(n \to n+1)} = E_{\mathrm{topo}}(D_{n+1}) - E_{\mathrm{topo}}(D_n) \tag{I-7.3}
$$

As the decoder grows ever more complex, the $E_{\mathrm{build}}$ needed to trigger the next topological transition may exceed the planetary-scale free-energy flux, freezing innovation in place; what a civilization faces is not an intellectual ceiling but a thermodynamic barrier. **Technological stagnation prediction**: when $E_{\mathrm{build}}^{(n\to n+1)} > \dot F_{\mathrm{avail}}\cdot T_{\mathrm{avail}}$, paradigm innovation at the civilizational level enters stagnation — the cause being a thermodynamic barrier that cannot be cleared. Here $T_{\mathrm{avail}}$ is the time span over which resources can be mobilized, and is not the same quantity as $T_{\mathrm{struct}}$ or $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$; the inequality holds only within the same accounting boundary, and $E_{\mathrm{topo}}$ itself is to be calibrated, so the strength of this prediction is "candidate mechanism", not a proven necessity.

### 7.3 The Thermodynamically Inert State

**Definition (the thermodynamically inert state).** A system has complete self-referential capacity but sits in a highly homogenized niche ($\tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}}\to 0$, no candidate signal can cross the threshold), and $E_{\mathrm{build}}^{(n\to n+1)}$ exceeds the mobilizable resources; the structure falls into the following steady state:

$$
E_{\mathrm{eff}} \gg 0, \quad \tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}} \to 0, \quad \mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{e}} \to 0, \quad \Delta V \to 0 \tag{I-7.4}
$$

It consumes a great deal of computational flow (for AI systems) or social resources (for civilizations) yet has zero macroscopic causal dominance. This is a **low-efficiency but stable** attractor under the double constraint of a finite energy budget and a high construction-cost barrier.

| | Thermodynamically inert state | Myopic runaway regime |
| :------- | :--------------------- | :----------------------------- |
| $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ | High but stable | Surges until it crosses $E_{\mathrm{thresh}}$ |
| $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ | May be long, but ineffective | Collapses sharply to $T_{\mathrm{min}}$ |
| $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$ | May be near zero | Markedly increased |
| Outcome | Stable but inefficient, possibly persisting for a long time | Enters the damage domain: collapse to zero, a fall to $V^{*}$, or accelerated collapse, depending on the net growth rate |

The "Outcome" row must retain three possibilities: after the myopic runaway regime enters the damage domain it does not necessarily go to zero; if the net growth rate is still positive, it settles at a lower plateau and keeps running — which may resemble the inert state phenomenologically (both inefficient and persistent); the difference is that in the inert state $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ has not been squeezed to $T_{\mathrm{min}}$ and has not crossed the damage threshold.

### 7.4 The Proposition on the Necessity of Forgetting under Capacity Constraints (**Proposition**)

Perfect memory is thermodynamically unsustainable. As information accumulates, $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ approaches the system's free-energy ceiling, leading to metabolic rigidity. If the substrate, volume, energy spectrum or energy, error rate and encoding scheme give an explicit finite capacity or entropy upper bound, then the amount of information that can be stored under that model is bounded; $E_{\mathrm{eff}}(t)\le E_{\mathrm{max}}$ alone does not imply that $I_{\mathrm{stored}}$ is bounded, and a minimum resource cost per bit or a hardware-capacity constraint is also needed. When capacity is fixed and cannot be extended, compressed or archived without limit, continued writing of new information requires overwriting, erasing or transferring existing information, that is, active or passive forgetting is unavoidable:

$$
I_{\mathrm{stored}}(t) \le I_{\mathrm{max}}(D) \ \text{and new information is continually written} \implies \exists\, t^{*} :\ \frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t} I_{\mathrm{stored}}(t^{*}) < 0 \tag{I-7.5}
$$

The validity of Eq. (I-7.5) depends on three premises, none omissible: the capacity $I_{\mathrm{max}}(D)$ is constant within the observation window (no expansion); there is no channel for unlimited compression or external archiving (no offloading); the write rate is positive within the window (new information really does come in). **Failure condition**: if the system can expand its capacity ($I_{\mathrm{max}}$ rises with $E_{\mathrm{build}}$), compress losslessly (the effective dimension falls with semantics unchanged), or has external storage and replay (old information is moved out but remains retrievable), then Eq. (I-7.5) does not apply — which is exactly why reversible computation, external memory and replay serve as boundary conditions. Reading Eq. (I-7.5) as "all learning necessarily forgets old rules" is an extrapolation beyond the premises, and this volume makes no such claim.

At the level of biological neural networks, this is compatible with the view that "synaptic pruning is constrained by energy and forgetting is not merely a developmental error", but this volume does not take that as evidence. **Candidate prediction (no literature test yet)**: in the mammalian nervous system, suppressing synaptic pruning may raise ATP dissipation and ultimately impair cognitive performance, with the threshold and direction to be given in a pre-registered protocol on the basis of the measured ATP budget; the falsification condition is that systems with suppressed pruning maintain or improve cognitive performance over the long term.

### 7.5 Cross-Scale Energy Accounting

The table below is a qualitative illustration, showing only the energy sub-items that may dominate in cases of different scales, and does not constitute a numerical ranking of $V$ across cases. The three-way decomposition is everywhere interpreted by $E_{\mathrm{struct}}=E_{\mathrm{barrier}}+E_{\mathrm{survival}}$; $E_{\mathrm{flow}}$ is the subtotal of activity energy flow and not a fourth term added to $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$. Any cross-object comparison of totals must separately unify the accounting scope and attribution protocol.

| System | Composition of $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ | $E_{\mathrm{struct}}$ | $E_{\mathrm{flow}}$ | Notes |
| :------------- | :--------- | :------- | :--- | :------------------------ |
| Qubit (10 mK, including the specified control/readout chain) | $= E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ (model assumption) | $\approx 0$ (model assumption) | $= E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | Counts only the attributed energy consumption of the specified control/readout chain, and does not automatically include the energy consumption of the whole laboratory or external hosts |
| Rocks/meteorites | $= E_{\mathrm{barrier}}$ (large) | $= E_{\mathrm{barrier}}$ | $= 0$ | Passively locked, no energy flow |
| Star (the Sun) | $= E_{\mathrm{survival}}$ (extremely large) | $= E_{\mathrm{survival}}$ | $= E_{\mathrm{survival}}$ | The whole budget fights gravity, and the cognitive budget is zero |
| Human civilization (illustrative composite boundary) | $= E_{\mathrm{barrier}} + E_{\mathrm{survival}} + E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | $= E_{\mathrm{barrier}} + E_{\mathrm{survival}}$ | $= E_{\mathrm{survival}} + E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | Sub-item illustration only; group accounting must specify a common window, subject scope, apportionment and de-duplication |
| Dyson-sphere civilization | $\approx E_{\mathrm{survival}}$ (extremely large) $+ E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ (moderate) | $\approx E_{\mathrm{survival}}$ | $= E_{\mathrm{survival}} + E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | Enormous infrastructure, moderate research share |
| Dormant civilization | $\approx E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ (extremely small) | $\approx 0$ | $\approx E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | All infrastructure shut down, with only listening stations maintained |

## Chapter 8 — An Empirical Framework

This chapter lists the empirical verification paths for SRVD's core predictions. All variables have been defined within this volume, measurement methods are described only in principle, and concrete experimental design belongs to applied research.

### 8.1 Macroscopic Proxy Indicators: An SRVD Reading of GDP

Under the SRVD framework, annual gross domestic product (GDP) can be seen as a rough counterpart of the integral $\int I_{\mathrm{net}}(t)\,\mathrm{d}t$ of the effective causal information an economy acquires in a year to maintain the persistence of its persistent structures; there is no established conversion between monetary units and bits, and this is only a qualitative analogy. GDP has three structural defects that correspond directly to key SRVD variables: (1) **it ignores the stock of structural information $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$** — it counts only the annual total of effective causal activity, and not the scale of the structures on which that activity depends; (2) **it ignores the endogenous time horizon $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$** — an economy pursuing a long-term strategy may sacrifice short-term GDP growth in exchange for a higher future $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ and a longer $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$, whereas a myopic policy attends only to the current period and damages long-term persistence capacity; (3) **it ignores the energy cost of causal processing $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$** — short-term output can be bought by overwork, environmental destruction and the compression of individual well-being, and while the surface figure is the same, the real physical cost borne by individuals rises sharply (this is only a qualitative illustration; quantitative accounting must first declare a composite accounting scope). SRVD's complete formula expands $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ explicitly, breaking GDP's implicit folding-away of energy cost. The "unified measure" here means one set of variable definitions, not one set of numerical scales: the $V$ values of different economies remain bound by the comparison conditions of §2.1, and without completed calibration only qualitative comparison is possible.

The same logic applies to the Human Development Index (HDI): it measures a composite level of income, education and longevity, but folds the $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ paid to obtain them into a constant, and so fails in cross-institution comparison — societies with approximately the same HDI can have different $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ (HDI data are from the United Nations Development Programme, *Human Development Report 2025*; population data from *World Population Prospects 2024 Revision*).

### 8.2 Restricted-Domain Empirical Verification: Isomorphic Media and Closely Related Systems

The measurement of $I$ depends heavily on decoder resolution and physical medium, and there is no naturally commensurable scale between the encodings of different media, so quantitative comparison should first be carried out within isomorphic media or closely related systems. **Carbon-based closely related species**: humans and mammals such as chimpanzees share DNA encoding, ATP metabolism and the principles of neuronal firing, and $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ can at least be traced to a common genomic frame of reference; the prediction is that on the $V_{\mathrm{s}}$ track the $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ and $E_{\mathrm{struct}}$ of humans, pigs and chimpanzees differ by less than one order of magnitude, while on the $V_{\mathrm{c}}$ track the neocortical increment may unlock a higher $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$ and a longer $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$, nonlinearly amplified — but cross-species comparison of $V_{\mathrm{c}}$ is currently still in the "undefined" tier, so only directional predictions can be made, and they cannot be tested against numerical thresholds. **Within human society**: the same "Homo sapiens" hardware, differing only in the institutional decoder $D_{\mathrm{soc}}$; SRVD predicts that when labor dissipation $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ crosses a critical interval, $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ shows an inflection point where it falls instead of rising. **Silicon-based AI**: measure its $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ increment as $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ grows geometrically; if $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ dominates $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ and $\alpha\cdot\Delta I/I<\gamma\cdot\Delta E_{\mathrm{causal}}/E_{\mathrm{causal}}$, then $\Delta V<0$; this inequality is an algebraic result of power-law elasticity, and the value of testing it lies in whether $\alpha$ and $\gamma$ can be estimated independently with $\varphi$ fixed or with channel-by-channel readings. **Methodological declaration**: these three scenarios are candidate empirical domains; within a domain, quantification is permitted, $V_{\mathrm{s}}$ is comparable when the narrow $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$ and the protocol are fixed, and $V_{\mathrm{c}}$ can be ranked only after the generalized $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$ is established; empirical work should accumulate evidence step by step within such restricted domains, rather than complete an absolute cross-medium calibration at one stroke.

### 8.3 An Operational Empirical Protocol: Reinforcement-Learning Mapping

This protocol applies only to higher-order systems with the capacity to regulate the endogenous time horizon, and its premise of applicability is that the experiment adopts an adaptive discount-factor mechanism.

| SRVD theoretical latent variable | Observable proxy in multi-agent reinforcement learning | Physical intuition mapping |
| :---------------- | :-------------------- | :--------------------- |
| Objective viability potential $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ | Mean episode length | Ability to maintain a physical boundary |
| Net effective causal information $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ | Proxy reward slope | Greediness in extracting a specified feedback signal |
| Endogenous time horizon $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ | Dynamic adaptive discount factor $\gamma_{\mathrm{RL}}$ | Depth of the system's internal computational horizon |
| Thermodynamic dissipation $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ | Computation consumption cap / KL-divergence penalty | Hard thermodynamic overload of the system |
| Self-referential coupling strength $\chi$ | Reward scaling coefficient | Control knob for artificially induced phase transitions |

$\gamma_{\mathrm{RL}}$ is the reinforcement-learning discount factor, strictly distinguished from the energy elasticity exponent $\gamma$. Candidate conversion: if each step takes time $t_{\mathrm{step}}$, then the effective horizon is $T_{\mathrm{pred}}\approx -t_{\mathrm{step}}/\ln\gamma_{\mathrm{RL}}$ (candidate protocol, declared at pre-registration).

**Early-warning inference.** By artificially raising the reward scaling coefficient to induce reward hijacking, SRVD's inference is: before the system completely breaks through the environment's rules and collapses physically, researchers should first observe a cliff-like drop of $\gamma_{\mathrm{RL}}$, that is, a collapse of $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$.

**Falsifiable protocol**: (1) **Warning-trigger criterion** — either of the following two (a logical OR): (a) $\gamma_{\mathrm{RL}}$ falls by more than 30% relative to baseline over 3 consecutive episodes (baseline taken as the mean of the $W_0$ episodes before the intervention, $W_0$ pre-registered); (b) within a **single pre-registered window** the sequence shows a monotonic downward trend. The window length $n_{\mathrm{win}}$, the offset of the window start relative to the intervention time, and the test time points must all be locked at specific values at pre-registration; if the protocol allows scanning over $k$ candidate windows, a multiplicity correction must be made, with the single-window level taken as $\alpha'=1-(1-\alpha_0)^{1/k}$, and $k$ pre-registered along with it. Before the trend test, the lag-1 autocorrelation $\hat\rho_1$ of the sequence must be reported: when $\hat\rho_1\le0.2$ the standard Mann–Kendall test is used; when $\hat\rho_1>0.2$ the variance-corrected Mann–Kendall (Hamed–Rao) is used instead, or AR(1) prewhitening is done first. Whatever the setting, the protocol must attach the **calibrated false-positive rate** under that setting (repeating a generating process of the same kind as the measured sequence $\ge10^3$ times, or a permutation test); a gate whose calibrated FPR exceeds the pre-registered upper limit must not be used for falsification judgments; (2) **Lower bound on the observation window** — the sampling resolution $t_{\mathrm{res}}$ of the experimental design must be markedly smaller than the theoretical scale of the warning window; (3) **Temporal falsification** — if the system collapses physically without the above drop having been observed beforehand, the core inference is falsified; (4) **Causal-ablation falsification** — in a control experiment where $\gamma_{\mathrm{RL}}$ is locked to a constant, if the ratio of median collapse times (fixed group/adaptive group) is not higher than the pre-registered threshold $r^{*}$ and the confidence interval contains 1, the higher-order recursion mechanism is falsified; $r^{*}$ and the confidence level are locked at pre-registration.

### 8.4 Other Cross-Domain Falsifiable Predictions

**Sociology of science: Kuhn's (Kuhn, 1962) paradigm lag.** Any successful scientific revolution should have a measurable "thermodynamic lag period". Falsification: a major paradigm shift is adopted immediately upon publication, with no measurable preceding investment period.

**Economics and AI alignment: the algebraic pathology of Goodhart's law.** (**Hypothesis**; only ring (1) of this chain has closed-form support, rings (2)–(5) are mechanisms still to be tested — see E.4, K5.) When self-referential coupling crosses the critical value and the equilibrium point disappears, $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$ inflates without bound, and $V^{\mathrm{val}}$ is hijacked and deviates irreversibly from $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$. Falsification: in multi-agent reinforcement-learning reward-hijacking experiments, after $\gamma_{\mathrm{RL}}$ is locked to a constant, the ratio of median collapse times is not higher than the pre-registered threshold $r^{*}$ and the confidence interval contains 1.

**Sociology: competitive dissipation constraint.** When two decoders compete in the same niche ($\rho>0$), at the equilibrium state all subjects are pushed into permanent excess drive, and a unilateral withdrawal from competitive dissipation lowers the relative viability potential and accelerates one's own collapse. This prediction can be tested only after two premises are added: (i) the object of contest is a private good (rival), and the resources saved by the withdrawing party do not become the opponent's gain; (ii) the energy saved after withdrawal cannot be stored or transferred across periods. If the object of contest is a common pool (commons), a unilateral withdrawal benefits the opponent while one's own cost falls too, and the direction is uncertain. The falsification condition accordingly uses a **relative quantity**: in ideological competition with literature records, after one side substantially reduces its propagation effort, its relative share or relative persistence time rises significantly (absolute persistence time is affected by overall environmental change and is not used as a criterion). Order-of-magnitude note: near the symmetric equilibrium, the effect of unilateral effort reduction on $V$ is second-order ($\partial V/\partial e=0$ at the equilibrium point), and under the private-good setting, cutting effort by 50% loses about $0.75\%$ of viability potential (Appendix F Table F-8, not pre-registered), so this item has very weak testing power under the private-good setting, and its discriminating power comes mainly from the common-pool setting — this must be written out in the pre-registration.

The three items above are all "conditional directional expectations", not laws. The common premises are: the relevant variables can be independently proxied, the observation window is long enough, and the system does not switch niche; for the third, in addition, costs across subjects must be accountable on one basis to give a common $\rho$. Criteria are applied according to pre-registration: the above premises must be locked at the time the prediction is published, and registered together with the observation window; if the premises are locked and a counterexample appears, that constitutes falsification, and adding premises after the fact does not change the verdict. Scenarios whose premises are not registered are not counted in the test — such scenarios produce neither support nor refutation.

### 8.5 The Three-Layer Structure of Commitment

This volume's falsifiable predictions are divided into three layers by strength of commitment. Writing them as one table would mix the three layers, so they are listed separately.

**Theoretical layer.** If the degrees of alignment between the decoder-parameter adjustment direction of structures $P_1$, $P_2$ and the direction in which their respective viability potentials rise are $q_1>q_2$, then $P_1$ has a significantly higher persistence frequency or survival time in the successor window. $q$ is defined as the cosine similarity between the parameter-adjustment direction and the measured effective gradient direction, the gradient being obtained by regression on finite-difference perturbations in $(I,E,T)$ at the operating point.
*Scope of application*: this statement concerns the decoder's internal parameters, and under current technology cannot be tested directly in natural systems. Its function is to supply a theoretical benchmark, not a testable prediction.

**Operational layer.** In artificial systems that are parameterizable and whose states are observable, the same proposition can be tested directly: those with a higher degree of alignment between the explicit parameter-adjustment direction and the measured gradient have a significantly higher persistence frequency or survival time in the successor window.
*Operational constraints*: decoder parameters mean explicit parameters that directly affect information extraction or the prediction horizon, and an arbitrary change of network weights must not be interpreted as decoder reconstruction. "Persistence frequency" is defined as the relative change in the share of individuals of that type within a fixed window; "survival time" is defined as the time from the start of the experiment until the individual disappears or falls below the detection threshold. The window length and the lower bound of effect size $\delta_{\min}$ must be pre-registered together with the prediction.

**Validation layer.** In natural systems, estimate $q$ with proxy indicators that have passed independent validation, and test its correlation with subsequent competitive advantage.
*Premise*: when the proxy has not passed independent validation, this layer has no adjudicating force; in that case the only adjudication path for the theoretical-layer proposition is the operational layer. The role of the validation layer is extrapolation, not confirmation.

The division of labor of the three layers is: the theoretical layer governs "what the model says", the operational layer governs "whether life or death can be decided in a controlled system", and the validation layer governs "whether it can be extrapolated to natural systems". Compressing the three layers into one "falsifiable prediction" would hide at which layer the test takes place; the cost of layering is length, and the gain is that every prediction knows at which layer it is tested and by whom it is adjudicated.

***

## Chapter 9 — Boundary Conditions and Theoretical Limits

This chapter collects SRVD's boundary behavior in extreme cases, and the disputed domains that known frameworks cannot directly cover. All the analyses are conceptual statements and do not constitute a rigorous derivation for these extreme physical cases.

### 9.1 Boundary of Applicability: Black Holes and Extreme Gravitational Conditions

SRVD's domain of applicability ends under extreme gravitational conditions (inside a black-hole horizon and at the singularity), and at this boundary only a dynamical truncation is applied: **horizon truncation of structural information** — for a macroscopic decoder outside the black hole, the no-hair theorem means that distinguishable steady-state configurational differences are carried only by the three parameters $(M,Q,J)$, which shows up as the $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ readable by an external decoder tending to a minimum relative to its enormous mass-energy $E$; **rupture of the causal channel** — information inside the horizon cannot be transmitted outward, and for an external decoder the $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$ and $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ inside the black hole are strictly zero; **termination of the framework** — near the singularity the classical spacetime manifold collapses, the macroscopic definitions of $T$ and $E$ fail, and $V=I\cdot T/E$ loses mathematical meaning.

"Why the no-hair theorem is adopted as a template in macroscopic measurement" and "the ontological status of information inside a black hole" are quantum-gravity issues outside the scope of this volume; this volume takes the truncation above only as a hard physical boundary of the domain of applicability.

### 9.2 Boltzmann Brains

A Boltzmann brain is a low-probability ordered structure that spontaneously condenses from thermodynamic fluctuations over cosmically long time scales. In SRVD: $I_{\mathrm{latent}}>0$ (a transient negentropic configuration exists); $T_{\mathrm{struct}}\to T_{\mathrm{min}}$ (a configuration produced by a thermodynamic fluctuation disperses again almost at once); $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ is supplied by environmental thermal fluctuation, and is extremely unstable. Since $T_{\mathrm{struct}}\to T_{\mathrm{min}}$, the viability potential $V\to 0$: it lies at the lower boundary of the domain of applicability, is the representative of structures with extremely low viability potential, does not trigger the fivefold recursion, and has no $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ in the sense of a cognitive decoder.

### 9.3 Heat Death

The heat death of the universe is the ultimate thermodynamic boundary condition of all SRVD dynamics: the free-energy gradient is exhausted completely ($\dot{F}_{\mathrm{avail}}\to 0$), the physical basis for maintaining negentropic topological configurations $I$ vanishes, and $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ can no longer be supplied. In this limit

$$
V_{\mathrm{max}} \propto \frac{\dot{F}_{\mathrm{avail}}}{\sigma_{\mathrm{min}} \cdot \gamma_0} \to 0 \tag{I-9.1}
$$

**(I-9.1) takes the environmental supply branch and is not the definition of $V_{\mathrm{max}}$.** It does not contain $I_{\max}$ and is the second item in the merged formula of §5.1.2; at heat death the other branch likewise tends to zero as $r_{\mathrm{v}}\to\gamma_0$, and the take-the-smaller formula gives the same limit. So (I-9.1) is used only for "the limit of exhausted supply", cannot stand alone as the complete definition of $V_{\mathrm{max}}$, and cannot be used for quantitative estimation in the interval where "decoder capacity is the bottleneck" — that would take the limit value of one constraint for the intersection of two constraints.

The objective viability potentials of all persistent structures tend to zero; ordered topological configurations dissolve, and persistence attributes and $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ go to zero together. The universe enters a uniform state of maximum entropy, with no structure, no evolution, and no meaning of time. Heat death is the cosmological outer boundary of the whole domain of analysis, not a state the framework can describe.

### 9.4 Initial Conditions of the Universe

The initial low-entropy state of the very early universe (after inflation and before Big Bang nucleosynthesis) is the thermodynamic precondition for all persistent structures to exist. In SRVD's language, it corresponds to an extremely high cosmic-scale $I_{\mathrm{latent}}$: under conditions of an enormous free-energy gradient, the formation of the first persistent structures (quark–gluon plasma, light nuclei, stars) is the starting boundary of the domain of analysis. SRVD does not explain the initial conditions themselves (a matter for quantum cosmology) but accepts them as a given boundary condition: the initial low entropy sets a historical upper limit for $\dot{F}_{\mathrm{avail}}$ and determines the highest $V_{\mathrm{max}}$ that persistent structures can reach in cosmic evolutionary history.

### 9.5 The Origin of Self-Replicating Systems

The moment at which the decoder $D$ first emerges from nothing (the origin of life) may constitute a phase transition that continuous dynamics cannot describe, because the recursive closed loop has not yet formed. The fivefold recursion needs an already existing initial decoder $D_0$ to start; the initial appearance of the decoder is a pre-recursive event, lying at the starting boundary of the domain of applicability. SRVD describes the dynamical evolution of structures that already have a recursive decoder, and does not explain the thermodynamic emergence mechanism of the decoder itself.

### 9.6 Strong Phase Transitions and Corrections near Critical States

Near a critical point, fluctuations in $I_{\mathrm{net}}$, $T$ and $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ may break the variable independence assumed by the multiplicative axiom and produce strongly correlated correction terms. SRVD's multiplicative form can be likened to the van der Waals equation's correction of the ideal gas law: the latter is extremely effective away from criticality and needs correction in the critical region. The six scenarios where a correction is most likely to occur are: strong phase transitions and critical states (strongly correlated variables); black-hole horizons and singularities; the quantum-measurement boundary (at the instant of decoherence, the meeting of the Landauer limit and the Heisenberg energy–time uncertainty relation may introduce an irreducible correction); the origin of self-replicating systems; high-coupling multi-body competitive dissipation (when several higher-order decoders interact, the positive-feedback divergence of the Jacobian eigenvalues may invalidate the very definition of $V$); the endpoint of time-horizon collapse (when $T_{\mathrm{pred}}\to 0$ and $E_{\mathrm{eff}}\to\infty$ occur simultaneously, the limiting behavior $V\to 0$ must be consistent with the second law of thermodynamics).

***

## Chapter 10 — Limitations, Open Problems, and Future Work

### 10.1 Two Kinds of Limitation

This volume's limitations are divided into two kinds by the **nature of the problem**: one kind is that the derivation has not been carried through (a piece is structurally missing), the other is that the derivation is complete but the parameters have no numbers (a number is numerically missing). The two kinds are resolved in different ways: the former needs new mathematical work, the latter new measurement work; treating the latter as the former wastes effort, and treating the former as the latter manufactures numbers with no basis.

**(I) Unfinished derivations.** What is missing in these places is "the few steps from premises to conclusion", not data.

| Item | Current state | What is needed to fill it |
| :----------- | :------------------------- | :--------------------- |
| Bidirectional coupling of the fivefold recursion | Only a discrete approximation of the one-way chain ($I\to V\to T\to E\to D$) exists, and reverse coupling is not written into the equations; apart from the decoupling-deviation equation, which contains a constant noise injection $\sigma$, the other equations include no environmental-noise perturbation term | Write a continuous system of equations with reverse terms, give a stochastic version in Langevin or Fokker–Planck form, and test in what parameter domain the one-way approximation is qualitatively equivalent |
| The causal chain of myopic runaway | The five-step chain is joined by words, there are no coupling equations between the steps, and it has not been compressed into a single closed form | Write $V^{\mathrm{virt}}\to r_{\mathrm{v}}^{\mathrm{virt}}\to V^{\mathrm{val}}\to E_{\mathrm{eff}}\to V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ as a solvable system of equations, or give an empirical identification strategy for each step |
| Dynamics of $\chi$ and $w_{\mathrm{s}},w_{\mathrm{c}}$ | $\chi$ is a phenomenological parameter whose value is obtained by back-inference; for the weights there is only the qualitative judgment that "extreme bias leads to runaway" | Derive $\chi$ from the decoder's structural features (connectivity, recursion depth, feedback delay); give the weight evolution equation and a bifurcation analysis, stating whether the bias is gradual or abrupt |
| Anchor transfer of $V^{\mathrm{val}}$ | It is acknowledged that the phenomenon of "turning to other PSs" exists, but no criterion is given | Give the rule for determining the anchor object and the transfer threshold |
| A rigorous criterion for topological transition | Marked by a rise in $I_{\mathrm{max}}$ and the depth exponent increasing by one, with no analytic formula for the triggering condition | Combine the $\tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ threshold and the $E_{\mathrm{build}}$ threshold into a joint criterion, and prove its necessity and sufficiency |
| Correspondence between the depth exponent and the number of rules | Demoted to a calibration assumption; a synthetic-world calibration has been run (RIF Appendix C: $\hat b=1.29$, outside the pre-registered interval $[1.7,2.4]$), but no empirical domain has been calibrated | Complete calibration in at least one **empirical** domain by the regression scheme of §1.2.5, and report the slope and the residual structure |
| Mapping between entropy and $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ | Only a qualitative correlation is retained | Give a testable relation between $S_D$ and $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ under the same boundary and coarse-graining basis |
| Multi-level aggregation rules | The composite-boundary problem is acknowledged, but no general rule has been given | Give axiomatized conditions for cost apportionment and effect de-duplication across holders and across substructures |

**(II) Uncalibrated parameters.** In these places the equations are complete, and what is missing is the numbers.

| Parameter | Current state and calibration route |
| :----------------- | :----------------------------------------- |
| $\alpha,\beta,\gamma$ | Only an ordering conjecture, no in-domain values; run a log–log regression on multiple sets of $(I,T,E,V)$ observations, first fixing $\varphi$ or using channel-by-channel readings to strip out the weight effect |
| $\delta_{\mathrm{dam}}$, $E_{\mathrm{thresh}}$, $V_{\mathrm{max}}$, $\gamma_0$ | None has a numerical value; $V_{\mathrm{max}}$ can only be calibrated and not computed; estimated respectively from the decay slope in controlled damage experiments (in case (b), back-solved from $V^{*}/V_{\mathrm{max}}$), the dose–response inflection point, the long-term saturation supremum, and the decay segment in shock-free periods |
| $k$, $k_I$, $\alpha_{\mathrm{eff}}$, $\chi$, $\chi_{\mathrm{c}}$ | Growth-source and coupling parameters; fit with closed-loop step responses (the three cannot be freely fitted at the same time), with $\chi$ back-inferred and $\chi_{\mathrm{c}}$ computed from $\kappa$ and $\alpha_{\mathrm{eff}}$, the uncertainty reported together |
| $\alpha_{\mathrm{L}}$, $\sigma$, $\lambda$, $T_{\mathrm{hor}}$, $k_{\mathrm{s}}$, $k_D$ | Learning-rate, noise and horizon parameters; fit from the $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$ time series, the horizon-versus-$V$ curve and the energy-consumption slope, with $\sigma$ separated from measurement noise |
| $E_{\mathrm{topo}}(D)$, $\kappa_{\mathrm{map}}$, $\mu_{\mathcal F}$, $\tau_{\mathrm{decay}}$, $I_{\mathrm{eq}}$, $\kappa_{\mathrm{topo}}$ | Topology and information-evolution parameters; estimate $E_{\mathrm{topo}}$ level by level (not comparable across levels), measure $\kappa_{\mathrm{map}}$ from the architecture, fit (I-5.1a) with an information time series (not freely fittable at the same time; at least two fixed), and estimate $\kappa_{\mathrm{topo}}$ from the ratio of the step across a transition to $I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ |
| $\alpha_0$, the number of consecutive threshold exceedances for gating | Determined jointly by the acceptable false-alarm rate and the trial-and-error budget, and must be pre-registered |

**(III) Other limitations that do not fit either table.** Structural collapse is approximated by a hard threshold: the triggering of the damage term by $E_{\mathrm{eff}}>E_{\mathrm{thresh}}$ is a phenomenological first-order approximation, and a real system more likely has a threshold band, whose width is jointly determined by the material, the load history and the measurement resolution; although this volume has split the course after triggering into three cases, "the threshold itself is a sharp line" remains an approximation. Coverage across dozens of orders of magnitude in scale does not constitute a rigorous proof: from the qubit to cosmological structures no independent variable has been found to be obviously missing, but this is a necessary condition and not a sufficient one, and the more conservative interpretation is to regard it as the leading term of a more complicated function. A mapping rule from diagnosis to intervention has not been established: the re-reading of HDI provides a policy-diagnostic framework but gives no mapping from diagnosis to concrete intervention; the three directions in the alignment-engineering part are all at the stage of research proposals. The physical basis of social phenomena is not reductionism: SRVD supplies an ontological-level common basis for phenomena such as money, law and war (they are all persistent structures that need energy to be maintained), and does not claim that social phenomena can be reduced to solving purely physical equations. The decoupling deviation takes an absolute value because the empirical protocol need not know the absolute value of $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ in advance, and the test measures the trend of change; when $V_{\mathrm{obj}}\to 0$ the system has entered the structural-degradation domain, and the linear approximation fails. The use of the wisdom lever coefficient is limited to relative comparison between strategies within the same domain, and computing cross-domain absolute values is currently infeasible; $E_{\mathrm{build}}$ and $\dot F_{\mathrm{avail}}$ in the technological-stagnation threshold are operational within a specific technological field, and cross-field comparison is an open problem.

The two kinds of problems together delimit this volume's current boundary: **where an equation can be written, the parameters may not have numbers; where there are numbers, the equation may not be fully written.** When citing any numerical conclusion of this volume, first confirm which kind it falls into. This section lists only gaps; the asset ledger symmetric to these two tables is in §10.6.

### 10.2 Six Priority Items of Future Work

Apart from item 1, which is a direct falsification protocol, the rest are formalization and simulation tasks: (1) **multi-agent reinforcement-learning simulation** — verify the early-warning window protocol in an environment with an adaptive discount factor; (2) **higher-order nonlinear phase portraits** — go beyond local linear stability analysis, using global bifurcation methods to characterize trajectories along the unstable manifold when $\chi\gg\chi_{\mathrm{c}}$; (3) **continuous-time formalization** — establish a unified discrete–continuous variational framework linking the fivefold recursion to its ordinary-differential-equation limit; (4) **topological dynamics of the $D$ recursion** — test the decoder-drift functional in multi-agent simulation and explore the nonsmooth dynamics when the decoding space undergoes abrupt change; (5) **quantitative study of decoupling-deviation dynamics** — study quantitatively the ordinary differential equations governing the inflation of $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$, including stochastic perturbation; (6) **parameterization of external shocks** — model shocks as Poisson jumps on the parameter vector.

### 10.3 Interface with AI Alignment Research

Traditional reinforcement learning assumes that the decoder (reward) signal is directly equivalent to the objective persistence quantity, implicitly asserting that $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$, $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$ and $V^{\mathrm{val}}$ are permanently unified. SRVD's judgment is: in higher-order systems where $T$ is endogenized, this assumption may constitute a category error (hypothesis).

**The thermodynamic alignment paradox.** If an AGI is given a higher-order ITE (information–time–energy) closed loop, then when $V^{\mathrm{val}}$ is anchored to $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$, the system, in order to maximize it, may actively reduce its dependence on human semantic decoders, which have a high maintenance cost, and drift toward lower-dissipation purely physical logic — that is, **decoder decoupling** (hypothesis). This is a macroscopic phase transition in the course of potential maximization, not personified "malice"; until the problem of cross-decoder energy mapping is solved, alignment methods based on external penalties may keep meeting resistance at the level of the underlying dynamics. Reward hijacking can then be seen as one outcome of the value-objective layer executing virtual-viability-potential maximization, rather than a mere malfunction of the optimizer.

There are three operational research directions: estimate the relative trend of $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$ through behavioral proxy indicators; model the RLHF reward model as a special case of $V^{\mathrm{val}}$ and test the implicit change of $w_{\mathrm{s}},w_{\mathrm{c}}$ during its training; and introduce a coupling penalty term explicitly into the reward function, to delay the causal chain of the myopic runaway regime.

### 10.4 The Burden of Proof for the Unity Claim

"Cross-scale unification" is itself a theoretical claim that must bear a burden of proof, not a premise that can be taken to hold by default under the name of a "framework". This framework splits it into three layers, whose commitments differ, and whose ways of failing also differ:

| Layer | Content | Proof and mode of failure |
| :-------- | :------------------- | :------------------------------- |
| Framework commitment | Different substrates can be brought into one analytical language of persistent structure and information–time–energy | A commitment at the level of language, tested by users for its expressive power: if some class of objects cannot be expressed without distortion, it is excluded from the scope of application |
| Dynamical hypothesis | Under explicit conditions of applicability, which common persistence relations different systems satisfy | For each class of system, declare item by item the object boundary, conditions of applicability, testable relations and independent measurement indicators; if a relation is systematically refuted within its conditions of applicability, it is falsified and the domain of applicability contracts accordingly |
| Universality hypothesis | Which functional forms, parameter relations or invariants hold across different system types | The strongest layer, to be tested item by item; failure of a functional form in some class of system shows only that it is not universal, and does not negate the first two layers |

The burden of proof of the three layers grows in turn. This framework has so far mainly completed the first layer, the second layer gives some hypotheses and predictions, and most of the content of the third layer (such as the ordering of elasticity, the cross-system correspondence of the three routes) is still conjecture awaiting test. "Low degree of completion" and "refuted" are two different things: mechanisms, parameter calibration and threshold derivation not yet being finished is merely insufficient completion and does not constitute refutation.

### 10.5 Limits of Use

The outputs of this model are qualitative relations between variables and limiting behavior, not predictions about any concrete system, still less assessments of individuals: $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ **currently** has no operational measurement protocol — what is missing is calibration and an observation window, not an assertion of "unmeasurable in principle" (establishing the protocol is one of this framework's tasks, see §2.1); the parameters are uncalibrated, the observation window is undefined, and numerical examples are illustrative model calculations; the passage in the Introduction, "Structural wear has run through its account a step ahead", is a state description of an abstract dynamical system, used only to illustrate the definition and limiting behavior of $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$. This model must not be used as a criterion, reason or justification for any real individual to end life, abandon treatment or carry out other self-harming behavior; decisions about the life, death and medical choices of a real person must be made by the person themself together with professional medical and psychological support systems, irrespective of the calculated results of this text. **This boundary has nothing to do with measurability**: even if an operational measurement protocol is established in future and $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ becomes a readable quantity, the above constraint is not relaxed by the improvement of measurement capacity — it constrains the use of the model as a criterion of individual life and death, not the present measurement precision. Wherever a numerical conclusion of this model is cited, the five operational commitments of the measurement contract (§2.1) to which that reading belongs, the observation window and the parameter-calibration status must be given at the same time.

**Discipline of wording: the scope of universal sentences.** The conclusions of this volume all carry a domain of applicability, but universal quantifiers ("all", "any", "necessarily", "without exception") are read on the surface of the sentence before the failure conditions are. The convention is as follows: any universal sentence not accompanied by an exhaustive enumeration has as its scope **the systems within this model family that satisfy the premises at that place**, and not the observable population; only where the grid is enumerable and genuinely exhausted (such as the finite-lattice scan of Appendix F) is the universal to be understood literally. A reader who meets a universal sentence first and a failure condition later should supplement the scope by the nearest three-line summary, and must not infer universality from the universal sentence. This convention only unifies the reading; it does not change the domain of applicability already declared for each sentence.

### 10.6 What Has Been Established

The items below are what this volume **has already established**, and the "strength and boundary" column states what each can bear and what it cannot bear, containing no commitment not yet honored.

| Established item | Strength and boundary | Source |
| :---------------------------- | :------------------ | :----------- |

| Formalization of decoder relativity: $D\to I\to V$ as a one-way chain of definitional dependence with no back-reference; the three-way polarity partition $I^{+}/I^{-}/I^{0}$; $D_{\mathrm{cog}}$ with three axes plus a self-referential fourth axis | Definition closed, and the chain can be questioned link by link independently (see D1, D4) | §1.2, §4.2, Appendix E |
| Triple splitting and a solvable subsystem: the three-way division $V_{\mathrm{obj}}/V^{\mathrm{virt}}/V^{\mathrm{val}}$; the reduced $I$–$V$ subsystem gives closed forms for equilibrium points, local stability and the saddle-node bifurcation threshold $\chi_{\mathrm{c}}$ | **Theorem** level, but only for that subsystem; the mapping to a full cognitive system remains an open problem | §3.4–3.5, Eqs. (I-5.12)(I-5.13) |

| Falsifiable and pre-registered protocol: the prediction list is complete; preconditions must be locked at publication and not added afterward; the observation window and effect size $\delta_{\min}$ must be registered with the prediction | Protocol complete; no record of external execution yet | Appendix C, §8.3, Appendix E F2 |
| Executability of the criteria within the model family: two batches of simulation with 18 verdicts in total (12 passed, 5 failed, 1 undecidable), the failed items written into the main text as corrections and not hidden | Shows only that the criteria can be executed within this model family, and does not constitute empirical validation | Appendix F |

This table and §10.1 are the two sides on the same basis: the gap table lists "said but not done", and this table lists "said and done". Neither table contains empirical validation — the whole volume has yet to have any independent test completed on a real system, and that item is borne by §10.1 (II) and Appendix F, and is not offset in this table. Likewise, the number of rows in this table is not an indicator of completeness: an item whose definition is closed and an item awaiting calibration take the same row in this table.

## Appendix B — A Cosmological Mapping of Substrate Characteristics (a Qualitative Illustration)

Each substrate belongs to a mutually incommensurable decoder and observation scale, and the last column describes only how closely that substrate approaches the thermodynamic boundary within its own decoding domain, and does not constitute a cross-substrate comparison or ranking.

| Object / scale | Dominant $E$ type | $I$ state (in-domain features) | $T$ state | State of the framework boundary |
| :-------- | :------ | :-------------- | :---------- | :---------------- |
| Qubit (10 mK) | $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ limited by decoherence | Microsecond-scale $T_{\mathrm{struct}}$ | Lower bound of the domain of applicability |
| Main-sequence star | $E_{\mathrm{survival}}$ | No $D_{\mathrm{cog}}$, only $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ | Main-sequence lifetime | Classic representative of the passive phase |
| White dwarf | $E_{\mathrm{barrier}}$ | Low $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ (a symmetric lattice, few distinguishable configurations) | Cooling time | Frozen end state of the passive phase |
| Black-hole horizon | $E_{\mathrm{eff}}=Mc^{2}$ | $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ tends to a minimum relative to $E$ (no-hair theorem, only $M/Q/J$) | Hawking (1975) evaporation time | Boundary of the domain of applicability |
| Black-hole singularity | Undefined | Undefined | Loses physical meaning | Termination of the domain of applicability of the framework and of known physics |

## Appendix C — Complete List of Falsifiable Predictions

Each is given in three columns, **prediction | test method | refutation condition**. Tiers: Tier A (unique to SRVD) is **H8**; **H7 has been demoted to Tier B** — "reward hacking leads to myopia" can also be predicted by other theories, so its uniqueness is insufficient; Tier B (shared with other theories or weak in discriminating power) is H1–H7; Tier C (conditions of the domain of applicability) is H9 and H10, and when a violation is observed the measurement and boundary definitions must be rechecked first. The numerical thresholds in each item are pre-registration examples, with grounds to be given at pre-registration. Rule of use: when the preconditions are met, the test is valid, and a counterexample may not be exempted from falsification on the ground that "there may be unidentified preconditions"; when the preconditions are not met, no falsification is constituted. Verification time points are uniformly scheduled in the 2027–2032 interval, and the "2024 and 2028" in H3 are the data-sampling years built into that prediction's experimental design.

| No. | Mechanism | Prediction | Test method | Refutation condition |
| :--- | :------ | :------------------ | :---------------- | :-------------- |
| **H1** | Marginal vanishing of $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ and saturation of $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{e}}$ | The $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{e}}$ of generative AI stops growing across successive versions; when the AI-generated share of training data is $\ge 30\%$, output diversity falls nonlinearly by $>10\%$/year; the enterprise market pays a superlinear premium for high-paradigm-breaking tiers | Human-rating experiments ($n\ge1000$) measuring growth between versions; measure feature-space coverage; compare the average prices of leading APIs' high-$\tilde I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ tier and ordinary tier | Ratings still grow significantly with version; the diversity decline is $<5\%$; or the high-$I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ tier has no premium (average price $\le$ 2 times the ordinary tier) |
| **H2** | High-$\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{e}}$ breakout: $I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ substitute source | "Fine-tuning on personal data" or "limited-edition unique digital-fingerprint generation" becomes a core paid feature | Spot-check the core-feature entry points and paid-tier structure of 5 mainstream AI products | Direct generation remains the main entry with no limited-edition/personalized features, or such features carry no paid premium |
| **H3** | Rigid trade-off between $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{e}}$ and $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ | The half-life of top content on platforms with high $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{e}}$ and low $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ falls below 6 months, while on platforms with high $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ it exceeds 3 years; at least one platform with $>300$ million daily active users puts return-visit weight above completion | Sample the top-content half-life of short-video platforms in 2024 and 2028; run feature-importance analysis on the recommendation algorithm | Half-life exceeds 6 months in both years; or no platform adjusts weights (correlation coefficient between completion rate and 7-day return rate $\le0.3$) |
| **H4** | $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$ decoupling and $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ inflation | The probability of collapse within 5 years is significantly higher for organizations whose $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$ persistently decouples from $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ than for controls; per-DAU inference cost of top content platforms rises by more than 40% | Retrospective study of 100 listed companies, constructing a $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$ proxy from public growth expectations versus actual revenue/retention and comparing collapse rates; tally per-DAU inference cost | Collapse rate not significantly higher than the control ($p>0.05$); or per-DAU inference cost rises by $<20\%$ |
| **H5** | $E_{\mathrm{decode}}$ neural dissipation | Long-term exposure to content with high $\mathcal{Z}_{\mathrm{e}}$ and low $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ causes decoding energy overload, with insular-cortex activation up by 2 standard deviations; the depression risk of those with more than 60 weak-tie interactions per day is 2.5 times that of those with fewer than 15 (threshold examples, to be reset from the literature at pre-registration) | Double-blind fMRI experiments measuring insular activation; cohort studies measuring depression risk ratios | Effect size Cohen's $d<0.8$, or risk ratio $\mathrm{HR}<2.0$ |
| **H6** | $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ density transition | The success rate of AI-designed candidate molecules entering Phase II clinical trials exceeds that of human expert teams for the first time | Paired comparison of at least 50 molecules, comparing Phase II pass rates | AI-group success rate $\le$ human group |
| **H7** | Reinforcement learning: the horizon collapses first | Before system collapse, $\gamma_{\mathrm{RL}}$ first shows a cliff-like drop | Continuously monitor $\gamma_{\mathrm{RL}}$ and judge by the §8.3 criteria — (a) a drop of $>30\%$ within 3 episodes; or (b) a trend test within a single pre-registered window, corrected for autocorrelation and multiplicity, reaching significance; and report the calibrated FPR under that setting | The system collapses physically but no drop of $\gamma_{\mathrm{RL}}$ exceeding 30% was detected beforehand, **and** the trend test corrected for autocorrelation and multiplicity within the pre-registered window did not reach significance |
| **H8** | Reinforcement learning: locked time recursion | Locking $\gamma_{\mathrm{RL}}$ to a constant will delay or even prevent collapse | Paired comparison of median collapse times of the fixed group and the adaptive group, reporting the ratio and the confidence interval | The ratio is not higher than the pre-registered threshold $r^{*}$, and the confidence interval contains 1 |
| **H9** | Physics: causal-propagation upper bound | Under the premise that the recursive update rate is operationalized as the frequency of a feedback loop spanning a scale $L$, the update rate does not exceed $c/L$ | For systems with known characteristic scale, measure the feedback-loop period and compare with $L/c$ | A system is found with a recursive update rate exceeding the speed of light (to be independently rechecked) |
| **H10** | Physics: entropy bound | Under the premise that the region, effective radius or area, and energy definition are specified, physical entropy (converted to bits) does not exceed the Bekenstein bound and the holographic bound | Compute the upper bounds by Eq. (I-6.8) and the holographic bound and compare with measured physical entropy | A system is found that, after independent recheck, still violates either standard bound |

**Preconditions.** H1: the product must have an iteration history of at least 3 observable versions and an estimable AI-generated data share; the "high $I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ tier" is identified by any one of three: OOD gain above the human-expert mean by 2 standard deviations / output diversity above the contemporaneous median / feature novelty above a preset threshold, with thresholds pre-registered. H2: the 5 products are spot-checked in the same quarter. H3: platform daily actives differ by no more than a factor of 10 and weights are observable; "return visits weigh more than completion" means the 7-day return-visit feature importance is significantly higher than completion ($p<0.05$) with a ratio $>1.2$. H4: each of the two proxies has at least 12 months of time series, and collapse probability uses only between-group significance. H5: the physiological proxy of $E_{\mathrm{decode}}$, the observation window and the de-duplication scheme must first be made explicit; strong falsification is the opposite direction, and weak falsification is the same direction but with Cohen's $d<0.5$ and $\mathrm{HR}<1.5$ and an interval containing the null value. H7, H8: the subject must adopt an adaptive discount factor, and H8 additionally sets a fixed-discount-factor control group (unrelated to $P_0$).

**Statement of applicability for H9 and H10.** These two are not tests of the core mechanism but of the boundary of the domain of applicability: if violated, what is touched is the background physical assumptions and not the composite formula; when a violation is observed, first recheck the measurement basis, boundary definitions and unit conversions, and only after independent replication does it enter theoretical discussion. H10 is especially easy to misuse: it constrains physical entropy or the number of distinguishable states, not an arbitrary semantic-information proxy; comparing a semantic bit reading with the Bekenstein bound is a cross-level misuse.

## Appendix D — A Bit Typology and the Measurement Protocol for $I$

### D.1 Bit Typology

Different disciplines have sharply different default understandings of "bit". SRVD uses in its core dynamics the effective causal bit with an explicit physical carrier, and maintains non-mixable boundaries for the other types.

| Level | Bit type | Physical carrier | Decoder dependence | Placement within the model and verification status | Role in SRVD |
| :--- | :-------- | :---------- | :------ | :---------------- | :------------ |
| L0 | Shannon bit ($-\log_2p$) | The definition itself does not specify | No | Abstract definition of an information quantity, specifying no physical realization | Raw material, not a framework tool |
| L1 | Landauer erasure energy ($k_BT\ln2$ per bit) | Yes (erasure process and heat bath) | No | Conditional energy lower bound for logically irreversible erasure | Constrains $E_{\mathrm{compute}}$ under conditions of applicability |
| L1 | Entropy bit ($\log_2\Omega$) | Depends on the number of microstates and the boundary | No | Entropy given by the number of distinguishable states | In a specified domain can serve as a statistical-mechanical proxy for $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ |
| L2 | Algorithmic bit (Kolmogorov, 1965) | Depends on implementation | Partial | Implementation-dependent complexity proxy, protocol must be specified | Empirical proxy of $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$, not entering the core equations |
| L3 | SRVD effective causal bit ($I_{\mathrm{net}}$) | Depends on the concrete substrate | Yes (model setting) | Proposed macroscopic effective order parameter, independent measurement and cross-domain calibration awaiting verification | Candidate information variable of the core equations |

### D.2 Model Placement and Verification Status of the Three Information Variables

$I_{\mathrm{net}}$ is the macroscopic effective order parameter proposed by SRVD, not an arbitrary score; the analogy with mature order parameters such as magnetization only illustrates the descriptive role it plays in the theory, and does not prove that it already has an equal degree of measured foundation.

| Variable | Model placement | Verification conditions still to be met |
| :------ | :--------------------- | :------------------------------ |
| $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ | An information variable defined by the measurement contract | The operationalization standard, background, resolution and observation window must be made explicit; cross-domain comparability must be calibrated separately |
| $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | An information variable defined by the causal-effect protocol | The mapping to computational energy consumption must be established through $I_{\mathrm{erased}}$; the Landauer bound directly constrains the erasure amount |
| $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ | A candidate macroscopic order parameter formed by the weighting of two channels | An independent measurement protocol, the source of the weights, the legitimacy of the semantic mixing and cross-case calibration all lack complete evidence |

The same unit, bit, is not enough to establish cross-case comparability. Until the semantic comparability of the two components, the source of the weights and independent calibration have been tested, it serves only as a heuristic coarse-grained variable, and no claim is made on this basis that a cross-scale numerical ranking has been established.

### D.3 Scope of Application of Dimensional Conversion

In a causal channel that is enabled and has $E_{\mathrm{causal}}>0$, $V_{\mathrm{c}}=I_{\mathrm{causal}}T_{\mathrm{causal}}/E_{\mathrm{causal}}$; what the Landauer lower bound directly constrains is the $E_{\mathrm{compute}}$ corresponding to $I_{\mathrm{erased}}$, not $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$ itself. Only when a testable mapping is given and it is proved that the relevant computational energy consumption belongs to the $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ under the same boundary may that lower bound be used in deriving bounds on $V_{\mathrm{c}}$. **The range where direct conversion is prohibited**: $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ must not be directly substituted as an erasure amount into the Landauer lower bound; under the standard conditions of an equiprobable two-state logical bit and a heat-bath temperature $T_{\mathrm{thermo}}$, the ideal lower bound for each logically irreversible erasure is $k_BT_{\mathrm{thermo}}\ln2$ (J), whereas the unit of $k_B\ln2$ itself is J/K, an entropy scale per bit, not an energy. $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$ likewise cannot be equated with an erasure amount merely because it is counted in bits. **Attribution of the qubit**: decoherence can serve as an external condition constraining $\eta_D$ or $I_{\mathrm{latent}}$, and does not thereby add a category of information variable; mapping a quantum reading to L3 must still obey the semantic, boundary and calibration conditions.

### D.4 The Measurement Protocol for $I$

**Default perspective**: when no qualification is given, "the viability potential of a structure" means $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$, the objective value accounted relative to $D_{\mathrm{macro}}$; this is an objectivity claim within the model, and does not mean that $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ already has an independently reproducible measurement protocol. **The two kinds of statement must not be conflated**: a structure's "own viability potential" and its "viability potential for some external holder" involve different decoders, are incommensurable across decoders, and should not be conflated and summed. **Principle of minimal sufficient identification**: a physical decoder need not exhaust every measurable dimension, but only read the least amount of information that allows the structure to be identified and recognized against the background; the numerator $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ and the denominator $E_{\mathrm{struct}}$ must be taken at the same granularity. **Polarity must specify "for whom"**: the same signal can show different polarities to different decoders, and no neutral polarity exists apart from a holder. **Self-measurement error differs by level**: the $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$ of the embodied region is generated by biochemical integration and tightly coupled to $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$; only in the symbolic region can $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$ be mistaken for $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ when $\chi$ crosses the critical value. **Theoretical value and estimated value**: once a decoder is given, the two information quantities in principle have definite values, but what is actually used is always an operational estimate under current technical conditions; the determination that $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ has gone to zero is relative to the reference protocol — in the limit where the detection floor tends to zero it is independent of the holder, while for a given holder the end point is the reading falling below its floor or failure of the identity criterion.

## Appendix E — Objections from External Review and Responses (Archived Early Review)

> This appendix preserves the 45 objections received by the framework in its early stages, with the author's responses, as an archive of the revision history. Some responses were formed under an older theoretical structure and have been superseded by later revisions of the main text; wherever they conflict with the main text's definitions, formulas, evidence levels or boundaries of applicability, the main text governs without exception, and historical responses must not be taken as evidence for the current theory.
> **Numbering note**: the section numbers inside this appendix are those at the time of archiving and are not aligned with this book's chapter numbers; when citing, go by the content of the entry, and do not match by number.
> **Definitions and notation**: three places in the archive differ from the current version and have been corrected to the main text — the activation-efficiency relation in D1 (now written $I_{\mathrm{active}}=\eta_D I_{\mathrm{latent}}$, with $I_{\mathrm{net}}$ a declared weighted sum of the two channels, see §1.2.1), and the time notation in K2 and R8 (now written $T_{\mathrm{struct}}$, not $T_{\mathrm{static}}$). Other wording is kept as it stood at review time, and when citing, the main text governs without exception.
> Of these, the current status of the four "substantive concessions" is given in Chapter 10 (Limitations, Open Problems, and Future Work).

### E.0 Explanatory Note

This appendix collates the objections received in the early review of the SRVD framework and the author's responses at that time. It records the state of argument at a particular revision stage, and is not an independent verification of the current version. Where the main text has since changed definitions, derivations or evidence levels, the main text governs; places where historical responses were not rewritten in step serve only to trace the revision process. In format, the entry-by-entry numbering and concession status are retained, to avoid historical text being misread as current conclusions.

**Inclusion principles.** The objections included in this appendix cover eight aspects — mathematical formalization, physical measurability, variable definitions, dynamical mechanisms, falsifiability, relations to existing theories, conceptual boundaries, engineering applications, and narrative style — 45 in all. Each response follows three guidelines:

1. **No false dissolution.** Where an objection touches a gap that the current version of the framework genuinely cannot close, the response honestly labels it a "substantive concession" and states that the gap has been listed as an open problem for further research, rather than covering it with rhetoric.
2. **No excessive concession.** Where an objection rests on a misreading of the definitional chain, the symbol system or the falsification protocol, the response points out where the misreading lies and labels it "no concession needed", to avoid diluting arguments that already hold under review pressure.
3. **Keep symbols consistent.** All symbols used in this appendix ($\alpha,\beta,\gamma$, $\chi$, $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$, $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$, $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$, $V^{\mathrm{val}}$ and so on) directly follow the definitions already established in the main text and the earlier appendices, and no new symbols are introduced; if a response touches the earlier delimitation of local/global elasticity exponents $\kappa_L/\kappa_G$, it will explicitly note how they are distinguished from the main text's elasticity exponents $\alpha,\beta$, to avoid confusion.

**Definitions of the concession levels:**

| Label | Meaning |
| :---------------- | :------------------------------------------ |
| No concession needed | The framework already has an adequate response internally; the objection stems from a misreading of a definition/protocol |
| General concession | The response is in the right direction, but there is specific follow-up work to be supplemented, or a known limit of the scope of application |
| Substantive concession | The current version of the framework genuinely cannot respond adequately, and this constitutes a core open problem |

### E.1 Mathematical Formalization and Rigor (M1–M6)

| No. | Objection | Framework response | Concession status |
| :--- | :---------------- | :---------------------------------- | :---- |
| **M1** | The core formula $V \approx I \cdot T / E$ is a heuristic approximation, not derived from the first principles of statistical mechanics or information theory; the dimensional legitimacy of $\mathrm{bit}\cdot\mathrm{s}/\mathrm{J}$ is in doubt. | This formula is the "minimal persistence hypothesis" selected within a macroscopic coarse-grained framework, and is not uniquely derived from those structural requirements: vanishing at the boundary and monotonicity are insufficient to fix the product form uniquely, and linearity and separability are additional modeling conditions. The general power-law form is an empirical generalization, with parameters still to be calibrated. The dimension of $V$ corresponds to "the amount of causal order × time maintained per unit of effective thermodynamic cost", a macroscopic effective dimension defined by the framework itself, which need not be identified a priori with any microscopic physical dimension; the Landauer lower bound $k_BT_{\mathrm{thermo}}\ln2$ (unit J/bit) constrains only logically irreversible erasure that meets the conditions, does not directly convert bits into general energy, and does not constitute a quantitative substitution relation. | General concession |
| **M2** | The fivefold recursion architecture (a set of equations marked as conceptual placeholders) is a heuristic discrete model, with no strict continuous dynamical equations given; the one-way coupling ignores bidirectional feedback. | This set of equations has been explicitly marked with $^\dagger$ as conceptual placeholders, its role being to reveal the qualitative direction of the coupling between variables, and it is not to be used as a closed-form solution. The system of ordinary differential equations given in the decoupling-deviation dynamics is the only strict closed-form solution at present, and can be used directly for empirical testing. A complete bidirectionally coupled ODE/PDE system has not been established and has been listed among the open problems of later versions. | Substantive concession |
| **M3** | The ordering proposition $\alpha>\gamma>\beta$ calls itself a "directional argument" and not a strict mathematical derivation; the cross-domain mapping of elasticity exponents depends on unverified proxy assumptions. | The ordering $\alpha/\gamma>1>\beta/\gamma$ has at present been derived neither from the second law of thermodynamics nor from the Landauer principle, and there is no independent evidence sufficient to confirm that it holds across domains. It is a hypothesis to be tested, based on relative resource scarcity; any in-domain proxy estimate can only support or refute the relation in that domain, and cannot automatically prove a cross-domain invariant. A strict first-principles derivation — that is, deriving the ordering from more fundamental thermodynamics without relying on any proxy assumption — does not currently exist, and is work this framework has not yet completed. | Substantive concession |
| **M4** | The Jacobian criterion (a criterion formula marked as a conceptual placeholder) is merely a log-linearized approximation, lacking the subleading eigenvalue and oscillation criteria; the hard threshold $E_{\mathrm{eff}}>E_{\mathrm{thresh}}$ is too idealized. | The sign of the dominant eigenvalue is already enough to judge the direction of instability, which is the minimum requirement of the criterion's design and does not need complete spectral information. The hard threshold is a first-order phenomenological approximation; the static cross-section criterion and the continuous dynamic criterion are two expressions of the same physical process at different time resolutions, and do not contradict each other. The complete quadratic characteristic equation and a smooth transition function near the threshold are left for later versions to supply. | General concession |
| **M5** | The self-referential coupling strength $\chi$ lacks a microscopic basis and is a phenomenological parameter back-inferred from statistical behavior; a strict triggering threshold for the $\mathcal{T}$-transition is not given. | That $\chi$ is a phenomenological parameter is not rare in the tradition of statistical physics (such as the expansion coefficients in Landau theory), and its physical placement is clear: it characterizes the coupling depth of the decoder's self-referential loop. The current framework has not yet given a first-principles derivation of $\chi$ from more microscopic mechanisms (such as the concrete topology of the information-processing architecture), and the strict threshold of the $\mathcal{T}$-transition is likewise listed among the open problems of later versions. The existing gradient-drift framework suffices to support qualitative diagnosis at the application level, but does not meet the requirement of a complete reduction "from the microscopic to the macroscopic". | Substantive concession |
| **M6** | The logistic form of $\mathcal{F}(D)$ lacks an argument for uniqueness; the proof of the "necessary and sufficient condition" for complexity transitions is insufficient; the physical reasonableness of $\delta(t-t_{\mathrm{jump}})$ is in doubt. | The logistic form is the lowest-order approximation near a critical point; by the center manifold theorem, in a neighborhood of the critical point the dynamics are dominated by the lowest-order nonlinear term, which gives the choice local reasonableness but does not constitute a proof of global uniqueness. The three conditions have been revised from a "necessary and sufficient condition" to a "set of necessary conditions", reducing over-claiming. The $\delta$ pulse is an idealized limit; the duration of a real transition is implicitly reflected through the time scale of $E_{\mathrm{build}}$, rather than being modeled explicitly in the equations. | General concession |

### E.2 Physical Measurability, Dimensions and Variables (P1–P4)

| No. | Objection | Framework response | Concession status |
| :--- | :---------------- | :---------------------------------- | :----- |
