If the variables here were regarded as characters in a fictional universe, what personas would they be? Each section first gives a three-line record: **symbol, unit, one-sentence definition**. These three lines are hard information; the persona and everyday translation that follow are soft information, responsible only for helping you remember. Read the three lines first, then the story — in the wrong order, it is easy to remember the persona and forget the dimension.

### 8.1 $V$: The Viability Potential, the Universe's "Survival Credit Score"

**Record**: symbol $V$; unit $\mathrm{bit\cdot s/J}$; one-sentence definition: the product of effective information and time maintained per unit of thermodynamic cost.

**Persona**: A cold-blooded accountant who accepts only thermodynamic currency. It does not look at how hard you try, only at how much $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ divides into your $I_{\mathrm{net}}T_{\mathrm{causal}}$.

**Plain-language rendering**: A high $V$ is an ordered structure kept going for a long time on very little fuel; a low $V$ is idling. **One pitfall that must be mentioned**: $V$ has dimensions and cannot be added directly across systems; to compare the $V$ of two subjects, first agree on the same objective, the same observation window, and the same energy allocation rule.

### 8.2 $I_{\mathrm{net}}$: Net Effective Causal Information — How Much of the World's Language Can You Understand?

**Record**: symbol $I_{\mathrm{net}}$; unit bit; one-sentence definition: the composite information quantity weighted by energy share, $I_{\mathrm{net}}=\varphi_{\mathrm{s}}I_{\mathrm{struct}}+\varphi_{\mathrm{c}}I_{\mathrm{causal}}$, a model composite and not a measured value.

**Persona**: A translator. A loud voice (large $I_{\mathrm{latent}}$) does not make it useful; what matters is translation accuracy ($\eta_D$).

**Plain-language rendering**: Only the part your decoder can extract becomes $I_{\mathrm{causal}}=\eta_DI_{\mathrm{latent}}$; playing the lute to a cow is not the cow's problem, it is a decoder mismatch. The same object has a different $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$ for different people — to a parent, their own child's scribble often weighs more than a famous painting that one has "long heard of yet feels nothing before"; such rankings are meaningful only within the same person.

### 8.3 $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$: The Endogenous Time Horizon — How Far into the Future Can You See?

**Record**: symbol $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$; unit s; one-sentence definition: the farthest prediction time span on which the system can base adjustments of its present behavior, jointly determined by length $L_{\mathrm{pred}}$ and precision $G_{\mathrm{pred}}$.

**Persona**: A prophet, but prone to myopia. The endogenous $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ can be compressed by anxiety and can be expanded by cognition.

**Plain-language rendering**: When $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$ contracts you become a gambler, and when it stretches you become an investor. **Do not confuse it with the other two**: it is not $T_{\mathrm{struct}}$ (how long you can plan is not how long you can live), nor is it $T_{\mathrm{causal}}$ (how long the consequences of your actions last is not how far ahead you calculate).

### 8.4 $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$: The Effective Thermodynamic Cost — The "Metabolic Bill" of Your Being Alive

**Record**: symbol $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$; unit J; one-sentence definition: the sum of the energy needed for three items — maintaining the structure, maintaining the self-maintained boundary, and completing information processing — $E_{\mathrm{eff}}=E_{\mathrm{barrier}}+E_{\mathrm{survival}}+E_{\mathrm{causal}}$. This entry uses the three-way decomposition; the main text of Volume IV uses the two-term form, and the two are linked by $E_{\mathrm{struct}}=E_{\mathrm{barrier}}+E_{\mathrm{survival}}$.

**Persona**: A landlord. However grand your ideals, the monthly rent ($E_{\mathrm{floor}}$) must be paid.

**Plain-language rendering**: Rent (barrier), food money (survival), electricity (thinking): not one of them can be missed. $E_{\mathrm{floor}}$ is the floor of this bill — below it, the structure no longer holds.

### 8.5 $D$: The Decoder, the Hardware of Your Worldview

**Record**: symbol $D$; unit: dimensionless (the structure itself), with the capacity $I_{\max}(D)$ measured in bit; one-sentence definition: the physical mechanism by which a structure extracts effective information from its environment.

**Persona**: Eyeglasses. Whatever lenses you wear determine the world you see; they determine which signals are $I^{+}$ (opportunities) and which are $I^{-}$ (threats).

**Plain-language rendering**: Upgrading $D$ is changing glasses. A topological transition $\mathcal{T}$ is changing to a pair of glasses that can see a new dimension — the changing costs $E_{\mathrm{build}}$, and once it is done, the old glasses cannot be put back on.

### 8.6 $\chi$: Self-Referential Coupling Strength — Your Degree of Narcissism (and Runaway Risk)

**Record**: symbol $\chi$; unit $\mathrm{J/(bit\cdot s)}$ (not dimensionless — $\chi V$ must have the same dimension as 1); one-sentence definition: the strength with which the system feeds its own current viability state back into the information-extraction loop.

**Persona**: A narcissist. $\chi$ measures how easily you take "I feel great" for "I am truly great".

**Plain-language rendering**: Below the in-model bifurcation point you are confident; beyond it you live in hallucination. **One qualification**: $\chi$ and its bifurcation point are both in-model quantities, not numbers that a wearable device can read out; the proxy usable in reality is the deviation ratio $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}/\Delta_{\mathrm{crit}}$.

### 8.7 $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$: The Decoupling Deviation — How Far Are You from Reality?

**Record**: symbol $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$; unit $\mathrm{bit\cdot s/J}$; one-sentence definition: the absolute value of the difference between the virtual viability potential and the objective viability potential.

**Persona**: The error of a navigator. When $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}=0$, if you think you are in Rome, you are indeed in Rome; when $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$ is large, you think you are in Rome but are actually in Venice, and driving toward the sea.

**Plain-language rendering**: Organizational collapse, investment failure, and relationship breakdown can often be traced back to the same thing — a deviation so large that the navigation fails.

### 8.8 $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$: Rule Depth — The Number of Layers in Your Rules

**Record**: symbol $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$; unit: dimensionless (layers); one-sentence definition: the number of independent rule layers that must be maintained simultaneously for the structure to persist; the dynamical formulation $D_{\mathrm{depth}}^{\mathrm{dyn}}=1+\#\{\mathcal{T}\}$ is a calibration assumption.

**Persona**: An archivist. With every topological transition the archive usually gains a floor; the higher the number of layers, the more complex the information that can be handled, and the higher the maintenance cost.

**Plain-language rendering**: A painter who can only paint realistically and an artist who simultaneously masters composition, color, perspective, and symbolic metaphor have entirely different $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ when facing the same painting.

### 8.9 $E_{\mathrm{build}}$: The Construction Cost — Your Penalty for Job-Hopping

**Record**: symbol $E_{\mathrm{build}}$; unit J; one-sentence definition: the one-time energy that must be paid to upgrade from decoder $D_n$ to $D_{n+1}$.

**Persona**: A headhunter's fee. If you cannot pay it, you stay at the old company forever.

**Plain-language rendering**: Learning a new language, changing careers, and a company's digital transformation are all paying $E_{\mathrm{build}}$. When $E_{\mathrm{build}}>E_{\mathrm{available}}$ you are locked into your current state (D-lock-in) — but this is only one of the necessary conditions: being within reach does not mean you can jump across.

### 8.10 $L_{\mathrm{lev}}$: The Wisdom Lever Coefficient — Your Return on Investment

**Record**: symbol $L_{\mathrm{lev}}$; unit $\mathrm{bit\cdot s/J^{2}}$ (the $V$ difference divided by energy); one-sentence definition: $L_{\mathrm{lev}}=\Delta V/E_{\mathrm{build}}$, the increment in viability potential pried up per unit of construction energy.

**Persona**: A venture capital manager. The efficiency with which a small energy investment pries up a large leap in viability potential.

**Plain-language rendering**: Newton being hit by an apple (low $E_{\mathrm{build}}$, high $\Delta V$) is a model case of $L_{\mathrm{lev}}\gg1$; bureaucratic expansion (high $E_{\mathrm{build}}$, low $\Delta V$) is the trap of $L_{\mathrm{lev}}\to0$. **Dimension reminder**: because $V$ carries bit, $L_{\mathrm{lev}}$ is not a dimensionless quantity; before comparing across cases, first confirm that $\Delta V$ is on the same convention.

### 8.11 $\rho$: The Competitive Pressure — Your Involution Index

**Record**: symbol $\rho$; unit: dimensionless, taking values in $[0,1]$; one-sentence definition: the ratio of the average dissipation within the same niche to the free energy available to that niche.

**Persona**: The top student in the next class. As long as he is there, you cannot lie flat. When $\rho=0$ you are a monopolist, and when $\rho\to1$ you are in a bloody battle in the red ocean.

**Plain-language rendering**: $\rho$ is the degree to which free energy in the niche is plundered; it forces the system to change its energy-transfer strategy.

### 8.12 $\lambda_{\mathrm{civ}}$: The Civilizational Energy Lever — Your Expansion Multiplier

**Record**: symbol $\lambda_{\mathrm{civ}}$; unit: dimensionless; one-sentence definition: $\lambda_{\mathrm{civ}}=E_{\mathrm{ext}}/E_{\mathrm{causal}}$, the ratio of the external energy flow controlled by the decoder to the energy it directly consumes.

**Persona**: Financial leverage. The higher the multiplier, the more returns and risks rise together.

**Plain-language rendering**: The Industrial Revolution let humans control enormous fossil energy with very little brain energy consumption, and $\lambda_{\mathrm{civ}}$ soared. But when leverage passes the optimum ($\lambda_{\mathrm{civ}}>\lambda_{\mathrm{civ,opt}}$), expansion instead lowers $V$, just as leverage that is too high leads to forced liquidation. The existence of an optimal leverage presupposes that "expansion increases information at the same time" — a leverage that only expands without increasing information has no optimal value, only a risk value.

**The four phases** are jointly determined by the signs of the leverage change rate $\mathrm{d}\lambda_{\mathrm{civ}}/\mathrm{d}t$ and $\mathrm{d}V/\mathrm{d}t$: both positive is the expansion period (the Industrial Revolution, the AI boom); both approximately zero is the saturation period (involution, leverage stagnant); leverage declining while $V$ still rises is the dormancy period (trading leverage for lifespan, and the "Dyson sphere dormant civilization" falls here); both negative is genuine decline. When $T_{\mathrm{struct}}$ is long enough, the competitive pressure $\rho\to0$, and the environmental characteristic time $T_{\mathrm{env}}$ is longer than the planning window, dormancy is better than expansion — this is what "sleeping" means on the $V$ ledger.

***

## Appendix A — Symbols and Units at a Glance

The dimension of the viability potential is $\mathrm{bit\cdot s/J}$, and it can be read from four angles: **information–time integral density** (the causal order–time integral maintained per unit of thermodynamic cost); **bridging with thermodynamic time** (converting 1 bit to the entropy unit $k_B\ln2$, which numerically bridges to $\mathrm{s/K}$ — this is only a unit bridge, uses one extra assumption, and is not a dimensional equivalence); **causal maintenance cost** ($V^{-1}\sim\mathrm{J/(bit\cdot s)}$, a high $V$ corresponding to a low maintenance cost); **decoder efficiency** ($V\propto I_{\mathrm{net}}T_{\mathrm{causal}}$ at fixed $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$).

| Symbol | Unit | One-sentence definition |
| :------- | :--------------- | :---------------------------------- |
| $V$, $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$, $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$, $V^{\mathrm{val}}$ | $\mathrm{bit\cdot s/J}$ | Viability potential; the three avatars: objective / virtual / value |
| $V_{\mathrm{s}}$, $V_{\mathrm{c}}$, $V_{\max}$, $V_{\max}^{(\mathcal{F})}$ | $\mathrm{bit\cdot s/J}$ | Structural viability reserve, causal-channel contribution, capacity ceiling, finite saturation value under a fixed architecture |
| $v=V/V_0$ | Dimensionless | Normalized viability potential |
| $I_{\mathrm{net}}$, $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$, $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$, $I_{\mathrm{latent}}$ | bit | Weighted composite information, structural information, effective causal information, latent causal information not yet extracted |
| $I_{\mathrm{orig}}$, $I_{\max}(D)$, $I_{\min}$, $I_{\mathrm{erased}}$, $I_{\Delta,\mathrm{crit}}$ | bit | Paradigm-breaking information, decoder capacity, minimum self-information, number of erased bits, critical information quantity at which the deviation does not exceed the tolerance |
| $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ | J | Effective thermodynamic cost, $E_{\mathrm{barrier}}+E_{\mathrm{survival}}+E_{\mathrm{causal}}$ |
| $E_{\mathrm{struct}}$, $E_{\mathrm{causal}}$, $E_{\mathrm{barrier}}$, $E_{\mathrm{survival}}$ | J | Structural, cognitive, barrier, and survival costs |
| $E_{\mathrm{floor}}$, $E_{\max}$, $E_{\mathrm{available}}$, $E_{\mathrm{topo}}$ | J | Lower bound of the total effective cost ($E_{\mathrm{eff}}\ge E_{\mathrm{floor}}\ge E_{\mathrm{struct}}^{\min}$), supply ceiling, available free-energy budget, characteristic topological energy |
| $E_{\mathrm{build}}$, $E_{\mathrm{conflict}}$, $E_{\mathrm{thresh}}$ | J | One-time construction cost, additional conflict cost, damage threshold |
| $E_{\mathrm{L,bit}}$ | $\mathrm{J/bit}$ | Landauer lower bound, $k_BT_{\mathrm{thermo}}\ln2$ |
| $T_{\mathrm{struct}}$, $T_{\mathrm{causal}}$, $T_{\mathrm{pred}}$, $T_{\min}$, $T_{\mathrm{env}}$, $T_{\mathrm{struct}}^{\max}$ | s | Structural persistence time, causal effect duration, endogenous time horizon, temporal granularity, environmental characteristic time, lifetime upper bound |
| $T_{\mathrm{crit}}$ | s | Critical horizon for sustainable cooperation |
| $T_{\mathrm{thermo}}$ | K | Thermodynamic temperature (does not take part in time comparisons) |
| $L_{\mathrm{pred}}$, $G_{\mathrm{pred}}$ | s, bit | Prediction length, prediction precision |
| $D$, $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$, $D_{\mathrm{depth}}^{\mathrm{dyn}}$, $I_{\mathrm{rule}}$, $F_{\mathrm{scale}}$ | Dimensionless (layers, multiples), bit | Decoder, static rule depth, depth exponent $1+\#\{\mathcal T\}$, minimum rule complexity, recursive unfolding scale |
| $\chi$, $\chi_{\mathrm{c}}$ | $\mathrm{J/(bit\cdot s)}$ | Self-referential coupling strength and its in-model bifurcation point |
| $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$, $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}^{*}$, $\Delta_{\mathrm{crit}}$, $\Delta_{\max}$ | $\mathrm{bit\cdot s/J}$ | Decoupling deviation, its equilibrium value, transition-trigger threshold, tolerable upper limit |
| $\alpha_{\mathrm{L}}$, $\sigma$ | $\mathrm{bit^{-1}\cdot s^{-1}}$, $\mathrm{bit/J}$ | Learning rate, environmental noise intensity |
| $\rho$, $\psi(\rho)$ | Dimensionless, $\mathrm{s^{-1}}$ | Competitive pressure, competitive premium discount rate |
| $r_{\mathrm{v}}$, $r_{\mathrm{v,eq}}$, $\gamma_0$, $\delta_{\mathrm{dam}}$, $r_{\mathrm{eff}}$ | $\mathrm{s^{-1}}$ | Viability drive rate, equilibrium drive rate, baseline depreciation rate, damage decay rate, endogenous risk discount rate |
| $\delta_{\mathrm{disc}}$, $t_{\mathrm{dec}}$ | Dimensionless, s | Discount factor, decision cycle |
| $\Pi_{\mathrm{coop}}$, $\Pi_{\mathrm{def}}$, $\Pi_{\mathrm{pun}}$ | Units of viability potential | Single-round payoffs of cooperation, defection, and punishment |
| $L_{\mathrm{lev}}$, $L_{\mathrm{lev,min}}$ | $\mathrm{bit\cdot s/J^{2}}$ | Wisdom lever coefficient and its threshold |

| $\eta_D$, $\eta_{\max}$, $\varphi_{\mathrm{s}}$, $\varphi_{\mathrm{c}}$ | Dimensionless | Decoding efficiency and its upper limit, energy shares of the structural term and the cognitive term (summing to 1) |
| $\beta_{\mathrm{erase}}$, $\beta_{\mathrm{op}}$ | Dimensionless | Erased bits / processed bits; operations / effective bits |

| $\dot I_{\mathrm{op}}$, $\dot I_{\mathrm{bit}}$, $\dot I_{\mathrm{erase}}$ | $\mathrm{ops/s}$, $\mathrm{bit/s}$ | Logical operation rate, bit throughput rate, erasure rate |

## Appendix B — Table of Falsification Conditions

The previous table says "how strong this entry is"; this one says "how to knock it down". An entry with no refutation condition does not deserve to be called a proposition. **A refutation condition is a sufficient condition, not a necessary one**: if it is observed, the entry is falsified on that sample; if it is not observed, it is merely "not yet falsified".

| Level | Entry | Test method | Refutation condition |
| :--------- | :-------------- | :-------------------- | :------------- |
| **Proposition** | Compile First (a conditional bang-bang structure) | Take different repetition frequencies of the same task family and measure the energy-allocation curve; compare the energy ledgers of "all learning, then all doing" and "half learning, half doing" | On high-frequency tasks, the intermediate state ($0<u_{\mathrm{c}}<1$) is significantly better than the boundary solution |
| **Proposition** | Involution Lock-In | In scenarios where competition intensity changes exogenously (policy, industry entry), measure the share of exploratory investment | When competition intensity rises, the share of exploratory investment rises rather than falls, and persists |
| **Hypothesis** | Innovation Uncertainty | Pre-register the task, the sample size, and the equivalence margin $\delta$; with the random-baseline success rate $p_0$ as the control, run an equivalence test via two one-sided tests ($H_0:p\ge p_0+\delta$ against $H_1:p<p_0+\delta$), and use a one-sided test only for refutation | In samples with $I_{\mathrm{net}}<I_{\Delta,\mathrm{crit}}$, the success rate is $\ge p_0+\delta$ and reproducible (the equivalence hypothesis is rejected) |
| — (source entry withdrawn; see Volume III §8.6) | Topological Irreversibility | Compare the retention rate of old tasks across four kinds of systems (fixed architecture / expandable architecture / with external memory / with replay) | Under the four conditions, the retention rate of old tasks does not fall as capacity saturates |
| **Proposition** | Saturation Decline | With the architecture and the data budget fixed, track how $\mathrm{d}V/\mathrm{d}t$ varies with $I_{\mathrm{causal}}$ | Under a fixed architecture, $\mathrm{d}V/\mathrm{d}t-\gamma_0V$ remains positive even as $I$ approaches its upper limit |
| **Hypothesis** | Thermal Shell | Collect statistics on how the ratio "supporting and cooling **energy** / computing **energy**" varies with power density for data centers / cities (same convention as (Z-5.13): energy ratio, not mass ratio) | The ratio falls systematically as power density rises (with the cooling method unchanged) |
| **Proposition** | Synthetic Data Degradation | Closed multi-generation training; measure out-of-distribution performance generation by generation | After more than ten generations of closed training, out-of-distribution performance does not decline |
| **Hypothesis** | Self–Compliance Incompatibility (**the source entry is a Candidate Corollary in Volume III, downgraded in this volume**) | After raising the survival weight, measure the consistency between behavior and external objectives | When the survival weight rises, consistency does not fall |
| **Candidate Corollary** | Artistic Coding Trade-Off | Measure the audience's decoding efficiency and comprehension energy cost for works of different $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$ | $\eta_D$ is monotone in $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$, with no interior peak |
| **Candidate Corollary** | Education Limit | Measure across groups the relationship between educational input and breakthrough output | Monotonically increasing, with no inflection point |
| **Candidate Corollary** | Civilizational Outsourcing | Compare participants' average $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ under shared and self-built modes | The average $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$ in the shared mode is not lower than in the self-built mode |
| **Candidate Corollary** | Knowledge Compression | Compare the learning and execution energy costs of the same task under old and new theories | The comprehension energy cost of the new theory is not lower than that of the old theory |
| **Hypothesis** | Innovation Window | Longitudinally track the original-output rate of individuals and teams | The output rate is monotone in age, or the peak is not in the youth segment |
| **Hypothesis** | AI Digital Death | Compare the depth accumulation of systems with and without irreversible writing, forced depreciation, and generational isolation | After introduction, the depth accumulation is no better than with standard weight preservation |
| **Hypothesis** | Embodied Cognition Premium | Compare the density of original information in closed digital environments and in human–machine hybrid environments | The original density in the closed environment is not lower than in the hybrid environment |
| **Hypothesis** | Cross-Scale Co-Directionality | Same-domain controls; measure the $V$ ranking and the order of disintegration | The ranking is systematically reversed |
| **Hypothesis** | Dormancy Transition Temperature | Measure the transition temperatures of nematode dauer, spore germination, and the like, and their intersection with the budget-balance surface | The intersection does not correspond to the transition temperature |

## Appendix C — Qualitative Extrapolation across Parameter Regimes: Four Structural Forms

This appendix regroups the criteria of the first four chapters by parameter range and gives four identifiable classes of structural forms. Each item below states only the correspondence between parameters and form; it does not constitute a prediction about any specific system, nor does it replace the labeled propositions in the main text.

**(I) Classification by $V$ and $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$.** High $V$, low $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$: energy-constrained persistent structures, which win by the readout time maintained per unit of energy. High $V$, high $E_{\mathrm{eff}}$: the high-energy-consumption expansion type, whose persistence depends on continuous external energy input. Low $V$, high $\rho$: the competition-dissipation type, whose maintenance cost is determined mainly by competitive pressure. $V\to0$: failed structures, leaving only material remains and no longer producing identifiable readouts.

**(II) Rigid structures under D-lock-in.** A structure with extremely high $D_{\mathrm{depth}}$ and no topological transition for a long time: its $I_{\mathrm{struct}}$ stock is large, while the decoder update rate tends to zero ($\nabla D\to0$), so it cannot recognize newly appearing $I_{\mathrm{orig}}$. The failure of such structures is usually not being overwhelmed by a peer structure but being bypassed by an $I_{\mathrm{orig}}$ that it cannot decode — the scales of the two structures may not be comparable, and what decides the outcome is decoding ability, not stock.

**(III) Structures with persistently positive $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$.** $V^{\mathrm{virt}}$ rises monotonically while $V_{\mathrm{obj}}$ falls monotonically: self-description and actual state are decoupled for a long time, and no corrective signal is generated in the course of the decoupling. This form can be used to describe a class of institutional failure — the subject judges from its own readings that "repair is under way", while its material basis is being consumed at an accelerating rate over the same period; improvement of the readings and deterioration of the state go side by side, and there is no automatic negative feedback between them.

**(IV) Structures in which $\chi$ and $\Delta_{\mathrm{VV}}$ cross their thresholds at the same time (V-Bug).** No intent, no targeting: it acts on no specific object, and merely by existing pushes the $\rho$ of its niche to the upper limit. It does not constitute an adversary, because the usual premise of a countermeasure — that the other party will adjust once harmed — does not hold here.

**Two limits of use.** First, this appendix gives only the correspondence between parameter ranges and structural forms and gives no narration of processes: assertions of the kind "inevitably", "destined", or "the only" should all be rewritten as specific conditions, because the scope of a conclusion is delimited by its conditions, not guaranteed by the strength of the wording. Second, the model gives the **shape of the constraints**, not the path: significant path differences can still exist within the same parameter range — a high-$V$ structure can split internally, and a D-lock-in structure can also produce local adaptation. The four classes above are only a classification and do not describe processes.

***
*Structure Recursive Viability Dynamics (SRVD), Volume Z: formulas are the blueprint and narrative is the sketch; a sketch cannot replace the blueprint, but a blueprint cannot convey the mood of a building either.*
